G (x, \xi) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {\varphi_ {i} (\xi) \varphi_ {i} (x)}{\lambda_ {i}}\tag{2.6.23}
$$

若 $\| \varphi_{i}\| \neq 1$ 則(2.6.23)改寫爲

$$
G (x, \xi) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {\varphi_ {i} (\xi) \varphi_ {i} (x)}{\lambda_ {i} \left\langle \varphi_ {i} , \varphi_ {i} \right\rangle}\tag{2.6.24}
$$

若存在 $\lambda_{j} = 0$ ，則(2.6.22)爲

$$
u (x) = k \varphi_ {j} (x) + \sum_ {i = 1, i \neq j} ^ {\infty} \frac {f _ {i}}{\lambda_ {i}} \varphi_ {i} (x)\tag{2.6.25}
$$

k 為任意常數也因此有無窮多解。如果線性方程式變為Sturm-Liouville問題

$$
\mathcal {L} u = \lambda u + f \qquad \Longleftrightarrow \qquad u = (\mathcal {L} - \lambda I) ^ {- 1} f\tag{2.6.26}
$$

則

$$
u (x) = (\mathcal {L} - \lambda I) ^ {- 1} f = \int_ {a} ^ {b} G (x, \xi , \lambda) f (\xi) d \xi\tag{2.6.27}
$$

Green 函數爲

$$
G (x, \xi , \lambda) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {\varphi_ {i} (\xi) \varphi_ {i} (x)}{(\lambda_ {i} - \lambda) \langle \varphi_ {i} , \varphi_ {i} \rangle}\tag{2.6.28}
$$

對一般的解析函數 $g(z)$ ，(2.6.21)爲

$$
g (\mathcal {L}) f (x) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {\infty} g (\lambda_ {i}) u _ {i} \varphi_ {i} (x)\tag{2.6.29}
$$

令 $g(\mathcal{L}) = \frac{1}{\lambda - \mathcal{L}}$ 則

$$
\frac {1}{\lambda - \mathcal {L}} f (x) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {u _ {i}}{\lambda - \lambda_ {i}} \varphi_ {i} (x)\tag{2.6.30}
$$

我們可引進路徑積分(contour integral)則

$$
\frac {1}{2 \pi i} \oint_ {C _ {R}} \frac {d \lambda}{\lambda - \mathcal {L}} f (x) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{2 \pi i} \oint_ {C _ {R}} \frac {d \lambda}{\lambda - \lambda_ {i}} u _ {i} \varphi_ {i} (x)\tag{2.6.31}
$$

令 $R \to \infty$ 使得 $C_R$ 包含所有的奇異點（固有值）則由(2.6.27)得

$$
\frac {1}{2 \pi i} \oint_ {C} \frac {d \lambda}{\lambda - \mathcal {L}} f (x) = \frac {1}{2 \pi i} \oint_ {C} d \lambda \int_ {a} ^ {b} G (x, \xi , \lambda) f (\xi) d \xi\tag{2.6.32}
$$

但是

$$
\frac {1}{2 \pi i} \oint_ {C} \frac {d \lambda}{\lambda - \mathcal {L}} f (x) = f (x)\tag{2.6.33}
$$

由(2.6.32)、(2.6.33)與變換積分順序得

$$
f (x) = \int_ {a} ^ {b} \left(\frac {1}{2 \pi i} \oint_ {C} G (x, \xi , \lambda) d \lambda\right) f (\xi) d \xi\tag{2.6.34}
$$

這相當於說

$$
\frac {1}{2 \pi i} \oint_ {C} G (x, \xi , \lambda) d \lambda = \delta (x - \xi)\tag{2.6.35}
$$

例題2.6.8. 試利用固有函數展開式求微分方程

$$
\mathcal {L} u = - u ^ {\prime \prime} (x) = f (x), \qquad u (0) = u (1) = 0
$$

之 Green 函數。

[解]: 直接計算得 $\mathcal{L}$ 之固有值，固有函數為

$$
\lambda n = n ^ {2} \pi^ {2}, \qquad \varphi_ {n} (x) = \sin n \pi x, \qquad n = 1, 2, \dots
$$

$$
\langle \varphi_ {n}, \varphi_ {n} \rangle = \int_ {0} ^ {1} \sin^ {2} n \pi x d x = \frac {1}{2}
$$

因此 Green 函數為

$$
G (x, \xi) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {2 \sin n \pi x \sin n \pi \xi}{n ^ {2} \pi^ {2}}
$$

Green 函數 $G(x, \xi)$ 也可以寫爲

$$
G (x, \xi) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} (x - 1) \xi , & \quad \xi \leq x \\ (\xi - 1) x, & \quad \xi \geq x \end{array} \right.
$$

其實兩式是一樣的，只是其中一式爲其Fourier級數！

例題2.6.9．試以固有函數展開式求微分方程之Green函數

$$
y ^ {\prime \prime} + \lambda y = 0, \qquad y (0) = y (1) = 0
$$

[解]: 微分方程之固有值，固有函數為

$$
\lambda_ {n} = (n \pi) ^ {2}, \qquad \varphi_ {n} (x) = \sin n \pi x, \qquad n = 1, 2, \dots .
$$

由三角函數之性質和 $\{\varphi_n\}$ 為一正交族，因此Green函數爲

$$
G (x, \xi) = 2 \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {\sin m \pi x \sin m \pi \xi}{\lambda - \lambda_ {m}}
$$

## 習題 2.6

1. 證明邊界值問題

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \frac {d ^ {2} u}{d x ^ {2}} + \lambda u = 0, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq 1 \\ u (0) = 0, \quad u ^ {\prime} (1) + u (1) = 0 \end{array} \right.
$$

之固有函數、固有值爲

$$
u _ {n} (x) = \sin {\sqrt {\lambda_ {n}}} x, \qquad \tan {\sqrt {\lambda}} = - \sqrt {\lambda}
$$

## 2. 證明邊界值問題

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} ((1 + x ^ {2}) u ^ {\prime}) ^ {\prime} + \lambda u = 0, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq 1 \\ u (0) = u (1) = 0 \end{array} \right.
$$

之固有函數、固有值爲

$$
u _ {n} (x) = (1 + x) ^ {- 1 / 2} \sin [ n \pi \log (1 + x) / \log 2 ],
$$

$$
\lambda_ {n} = \frac {1}{4} + \left(\frac {n \pi}{\log 2}\right) ^ {2}, \qquad n = 1, 2, \dots
$$

## 3. 試求邊界值問題之 Green 函數

$$
\mathrm{(a)} u ^ {\prime \prime} = - f (x), \quad u (0) = u ^ {\prime} (1) = 0
$$

$$
\mathrm{(b)} u ^ {\prime \prime} = - f (x), \quad u (- 1) = u (1) = 0
$$

[解答]:

$$
\begin{array}{l} G (x, \xi) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} x, & x \leq \xi \\ \xi , & x > \xi \end{array} \right. \\ G (x, \xi) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} - \frac {1}{2} (\xi - x + x \xi - 1), & x \leq \xi \\ - \frac {1}{2} (x - \xi + \xi x - 1), & x > \xi \end{array} \right. \end{array}
$$

4. 證明微分方程

$$
\ddot {x} + w ^ {2} x = f (t), \qquad x (0) = x _ {0}, \qquad \dot {x} (0) = x _ {1}, \qquad t \geq 0
$$

的解可以表示爲

$$
x (t) = x _ {1} \frac {\sin w t}{w} + x _ {0} \cos w t + \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} G (t, \tau) f (\tau) d \tau
$$

其中Green函數爲

$$
G (t, \tau) = \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} \frac {\sin w (t - \tau)}{w}, & \quad \tau <   t \\ 0, & \quad \tau \geq t \end{array} \right.
$$

5. 考慮微分算子 $L = -\frac{d^{2}}{dx^{2}}$ 、0 < x < 1，試求 $\delta$ 函數在底下邊界條件之固有函數展開式

$$
\text {(a)} u (0) = u (1) = 0, \quad \delta (x - \xi) = 2 \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \sin n \pi x \sin n \pi \xi
$$

$$
\mathrm{(b)} u ^ {\prime} (0) = u ^ {\prime} (1) = 0, \qquad \delta (x - \xi) = 1 + 2 \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \cos n \pi x \cos n \pi \xi
$$

$$
(c) u (0) = u ^ {\prime} (1) = 0, \quad \delta (x - \xi) = 2 \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \sin \left(n + \frac {1}{2}\right) \pi x \sin \left(n + \frac {1}{2}\right) \pi \xi
$$

$$
\text {(d)} u (0) = u (1) \cdot u ^ {\prime} (0) = u ^ {\prime} (1),
$$

$$
\delta (x - \xi) = 1 + 2 \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} (\cos 2 n \pi x \cos 2 n \pi \xi + \sin 2 n \pi x \sin 2 n \pi \xi)
$$

6. 請將前一題推廣為 $a < x < b$ 。Hint: $\delta\left(\frac{x - \xi}{b - a}\right) = (b - a)\delta(x - \xi)$ 

## 第三章 Fourier 級數之應用

『於是我看到，與熱有關的所有現象都只依賴於極少數的一般而簡單的事實，使得每個這類問題都能用數學分析的語言來陳述。』

—《熱的解析理論》J. Fourier, 1768–1830 —

偏微分方程論就是起源於微積分對弦的振動問題的應用。一般將達朗貝爾1747年發表的論文《張緊的弦振動時形成的曲線的研究》(Recherches sur la courbe que forme une corde tendue mise en vibration, Histoire de l'Academie Royale, Berlin, 3.1747. pp.214 219)看成為偏微分方程論的發端。雖然在達朗貝爾之前，泰勒和約翰·伯努利等人也曾對弦振動進行過數學描述，但他們均未採用偏導數概念。達朗貝爾在上述論文中則首次明確導出了偏微分方程

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} u}{\partial t ^ {2}} = c ^ {2} \frac {\partial^ {2} u}{\partial x ^ {2}}
$$

及其解

$$
u (x, t) = f (x - c t) + g (x + c t)
$$

這個解就是一般通稱的d'Alembert公式。在達朗貝爾上述論文發表後不久，歐拉也發表了《論弦的振動》（見Nova Acta Erud.pp.512 527,1749）。歐拉沿用達朗貝爾的方法，但允許初始函數為他所說的”不連續曲線”（相當於今天導數有間斷的連續函數）。大約同一時期，丹尼爾·伯努利(Daniel Bernoulli,1700 1782)亦發表了自己關於弦振動問題的研究論文(1753)，他採取了與達朗貝爾和歐拉不同的途經，並斷言所有可能的初始函數都可表成三角級數的形式。那到底怎樣的函數才可以寫成三角函數呢？這立即遭到歐拉和達朗貝爾的反對，接著法國數學家Lagrange也都涉入這個著名的學術論戰，他們圍繞用三角級數表示任意函數這一重要問題展開了激烈爭論，這場爭論牽涉了許多數學家，一直延續到19世紀初，整個情勢似乎是對Daniel Bernoulli不利，直到後來法國數學家Fourier因爲研究熱傳導方程，將Euler、Daniel Bernoulli等人的三角級數理論發揚光大，並發展成一般理論。因此人們將三角級數稱爲Fourier級數，在這裡Fourier將百年來關於波動方程論戰的關鍵加以釐清，最後並還給Daniel Bernoulli一個公道。「幾乎所有的函數都可以表示爲Fourier級數」。

傅立葉應用三角級數求解熱傳導方程，同時為了處理無窮區域的熱傳導問題又導出了現在所稱的“傅立葉積分”，這一切都極大地推動了偏微分方程邊值問題的研究。然而傅立葉的工作意義遠不止此，它迫使人們對函數概念作修正、推廣，特別是引起了對不連續函數的探討；三角級數收斂性問題更刺激了集合論的誕生。因此，《熱的解析理論》影響了整個19世紀分析嚴格化的進程。

綜觀，由於研究波動方程而引起的科學界長達百年之論戰，基本的爭論觀點就是「函數是什麼？」因爲這樣的論戰讓人更清楚函數之本質，對於函數概念及整個分析的嚴格化產生了深遠的影響關於這部分的歷史，讀者可以參考《微積分的發展歷史》Edward原著(凡異出版社)第11章。

## § 3.1 熱傳導方程式

熱傳導方程

$$
\partial_ {t} u = k \Delta u, \quad \Delta = \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {j} ^ {2}}
$$

在偏微分方程及其應用佔有重要地位。在熱傳導理論，擴散理論、布朗運動及物理學的其他部門都會遇見熱傳導方程。它是拋物型方程這一類偏微分方程中最簡單的代表。熱傳導方程解的某些性質與Laplace方程解的性質相類似。熱傳導方程是Fourier 1822年在其名著《熱的解析理論》中最先引進並加以研究。對於整個偏微分方程理論之發展扮演著重要且關鍵的角色。

例題3.1.1.（齊次熱傳導方程式）

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t} = k u _ {x x}, \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = u (L, t) = 0,
$$

$$
t > 0
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = f (x)
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/5beee0915cd223f6fd7d37521a2ca581b08db836c8d4e0ea44ff3123d5ff6cea.jpg)



圖3.1.1


[解]: 假設 $u(x,t)$ 可寫為 $u(x,t)=T(t)\varphi(x)$ ，直接微分後代原方程式得

$$
\frac {T ^ {\prime} (t)}{k T (t)} = \frac {\varphi^ {\prime \prime} (x)}{\varphi (x)} \equiv - \lambda
$$

其中 $\lambda$ 為一待求之常數，其意義是固有值 (eigenvalue)，因此我們將一個偏微分方程的問題化為二個常微分方程的固有值問題，其實這就是所謂的 Sturm-

Liouville 問題

$$
T ^ {\prime} (t) + \lambda k T (t) = 0, \qquad t > 0
$$

$$
\varphi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + \lambda \varphi (x) = 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

邊界條件為

$$
T (t) \varphi (0) = 0, \qquad T (t) \varphi (L) = 0, \qquad t > 0
$$

我們當然不期待 $T(t) \equiv 0$ 之解，故可要求

$$
\varphi (0) = \varphi (L) = 0
$$

利用常微分方程的理論得固有值與固有函數

$$
\lambda_ {n} = (\frac {n \pi}{L}) ^ {2}, \qquad n = 1, 2, 3, \dots
$$

$$
\varphi_ {n} (x) = \sin {\sqrt {\lambda_ {n}}} x = \sin {\frac {n \pi x}{L}}, \qquad n = 1, 2, 3, \dots
$$

此時對應的 $T_{n}$ 為

$$
T _ {n} ^ {\prime} (t) + \lambda_ {n} k T _ {n} (t) = 0 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad T _ {n} (t) = e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} k t}
$$

即

$$
u _ {n} (x, t) = T _ {n} (t) \varphi_ {n} (x) = e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} k t} \varphi_ {n} (x)
$$

利用重疊原理(linear)， $u(x,t)$ 可表為 $u_{n}$ 的線性組合：

$$
u (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} u _ {n} (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} k t} \varphi_ {n} (x), \qquad b _ {n} \in \mathbb {R}
$$

現在要考慮的是係數 $b_{n}$ 怎麼決定？由起初值知

$$
u (x, 0) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} u _ {n} (x, 0) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} \varphi_ {n} (x) = f (x)
$$

另外由 $\varphi_{n}(x)$ 之正交性可知

$$
\int_ {0} ^ {L} \varphi_ {m} (x) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x = \int_ {0} ^ {L} \sin {\frac {m \pi x}{L}} \sin {\frac {n \pi x}{L}} d x = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 0, & m \neq n \\ \frac {L}{2}, & m = n \end{array} \right.
$$

因此

$$
b _ {n} = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} f (x) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x
$$

故 $u$ 可表爲

$$
u (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} t} \varphi_ {n} (x)
$$

[註解]:

(1) 因爲這個問題只有兩個變數 $x$ 、 $t$ ，所以偏微分方程經由分離變數之後化為兩個常微分方程，如果有 $n$ 個變數，則化為 $n$ 個常微分方程。

(2) 將初始值 $f(x)$ 表為固有函數 $\varphi_{n}, n = 1, 2, \cdots$ 之線性組合 (linear combination)，即將 $f(x)$ 表為固有函數 $\{\varphi_{n}\}$ 之 Fourier 級數 (Fourier series)，這個精神就是逆變換 (inverse transform)。

(3) 將固有函數 $\varphi_{n}$ 單位化 (normalized) $\widetilde{\varphi}_{n} = \frac{\varphi_{n}}{\sqrt{L / 2}}, n = 1,2,\dots$ 則

$$
u (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} t} \varphi_ {n} (x) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} t} \langle f, \widetilde {\varphi} _ {n} \rangle \widetilde {\varphi} _ {n} (x)
$$

其中內積定義為

$$
\langle f, \widetilde {\varphi} _ {n} \rangle \equiv \int_ {0} ^ {L} f (x) \widetilde {\varphi} _ {n} (x) d x
$$

爲了便於推廣與辨認我們定義

$$
E _ {n} (f (x)) \equiv \langle f, \widetilde {\varphi} _ {n} \rangle \widetilde {\varphi} _ {n} (x)
$$

$E_{n}$ 便是第 $n^{th}$ 固有函數上之投影，因此

$$
u (x, t) = e ^ {t \partial_ {x x}} f (x) \equiv \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} t} E _ {n} (f (x))
$$

$$
\partial_ {x x} \equiv \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} - \lambda_ {n} E _ {n} (\cdot)
$$

而這正是有限維值譜定理之推廣。

(4) 邊界條件 $u(0, t) = u(L, t) = 0$ ，告訴我們 $u$ 必然是一奇函數，所以 Fourier 係數 $a_n = 0$ 。

(5) 簡單的量綱分析得

$$
[ u _ {t} ] = [ k u _ {x x} ] \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ k ] = \frac {\mathrm{L} ^ {2}}{\mathrm{T}}
$$

$$
u (x, 0) = f (x) \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ f ] = [ u ]
$$

$$
[ \varphi_ {n} ] = \left[ \sin {\frac {n \pi x}{L}} \right] = 1 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ n ] ^ {- 1} = [ x ] = [ \pi ] = [ L ] = \mathrm{L}
$$

$$
[ e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} k t} ] = 1 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ \lambda_ {n} ] = [ k t ] ^ {- 1} = \mathrm{L} ^ {- 2}
$$

$$
[ u ] = [ b _ {n} ] = \frac {1}{[ L ]} [ f ] [ x ] = [ f ]
$$

時間 $t$ 與空間 $x$ 是透過熱傳導係數連接，且 $[k] = \mathrm{L}^2\mathrm{T}^{-1}$ ，而且 $t$ 是 $x$ 的二次

函數，即一拋物線。也因此，我們稱熱傳導方程是拋物型方程。

例題3.1.2.（熱傳導方程的混合型邊界值問題）

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t} = k u _ {x x}, \qquad - L <   x <   L, \qquad t > 0
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (- L, t) = u (L, t), \qquad u _ {x} (- L, t) = u _ {x} (L, t), \qquad t > 0
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = f (x), \qquad - L <   x <   L
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/1f88b6e9ebe26a2bc7ca3474265f8d09d1c3b8738cfb0f1154935b0c5eed17b0.jpg)



圖 3.1.2


[解]: 由分離變數可得

$$
\varphi^ {\prime \prime} + \lambda \varphi = 0, \qquad - L <   x <   L
$$

$$
\varphi (- L) = \varphi (L), \qquad \varphi^ {\prime} (- L) = \varphi^ {\prime} (L)
$$

其一般解爲

$$
\varphi = A \cos x \sqrt {\lambda} + B \frac {\sin x \sqrt {\lambda}}{\sqrt {\lambda}}
$$

$\varphi$ 必須滿足邊界值

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left[ A \cos (- L \sqrt {\lambda}) + B \frac {\sin (- L \sqrt {\lambda})}{\sqrt {\lambda}} \right] - \left[ A \cos (L \sqrt {\lambda}) + B \frac {\sin (L \sqrt {\lambda})}{\sqrt {\lambda}} \right] = 0} \\ & {\left[ - A \sqrt {\lambda} \sin (- L \sqrt {\lambda}) + B \cos (- L \sqrt {\lambda}) \right] - \left[ - A \sqrt {\lambda} \sin (L \sqrt {\lambda}) + B \cos (L \sqrt {\lambda}) \right] = 0} \end{array}
$$

或改寫爲

$$
0 \cdot A - 2 \frac {\sin L \sqrt {\lambda}}{\sqrt {\lambda}} \cdot B = 0
$$

$$
2 \lambda \frac {\sin L \sqrt {\lambda}}{\sqrt {\lambda}} \cdot A + 0 \cdot A = 0
$$

由聯立方程組之理論，如果期望得到非零解則行列式必須等於零

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c} 0 & - 2 \frac {\sin L \sqrt {\lambda}}{\sqrt {\lambda}} \\ 2 \lambda \frac {\sin L \sqrt {\lambda}}{\sqrt {\lambda}} & 0 \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

其解爲(即固有值)

$$
4 \lambda \left(\frac {\sin L \sqrt {\lambda}}{\sqrt {\lambda}}\right) ^ {2} = 0 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad \lambda_ {n} = \left(\frac {n \pi}{L}\right) ^ {2}, \qquad n = 0, 1, 2, \dots
$$

固有函數則為(利用聯立方程組)

$$
\begin{array}{l l} \lambda = \lambda_ {0} = 0 \quad \Longrightarrow & \varphi_ {0} = 1 \\ \lambda = \lambda_ {n} = \left(\frac {n \pi}{L}\right) ^ {2} & \Longrightarrow \left\{ \begin{array}{l} \varphi_ {n, 1} (x) = \cos \frac {n \pi x}{L} \\ \varphi_ {n, 2} (x) = \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} \end{array} \right. \end{array}
$$

$\varphi_{n,i}$ 顯然具有正交之性質

$$
\int_ {- L} ^ {L} \varphi_ {n, 1} (x) \varphi_ {n, 2} (x) d x = \int_ {- L} ^ {L} \cos {\frac {n \pi x}{L}} \sin {\frac {n \pi x}{L}} d x = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {- L} ^ {L} \sin {\frac {2 n \pi x}{L}} d x = 0
$$

而且

$$
\int_ {- L} ^ {L} \varphi_ {0} ^ {2} (x) d x = 2 L
$$

$$
\int_ {- L} ^ {L} \varphi_ {n, 1} ^ {2} (x) d x = \int_ {- L} ^ {L} \varphi_ {n, 2} ^ {2} (x) d x = L, \qquad n \geq 1
$$

因此熱傳導方程之解爲

$$
u (x, t) = \frac {1}{2} a _ {0} + \sum_ {u = 1} ^ {\infty} e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} k t} \left(a _ {n} \cos \frac {n \pi x}{L} + b _ {n} \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}\right)
$$

係數 $a_{n}$ 、 $b_{n}$ 可由初始值 $f(x)$ 決定

$$
f (x) = \frac {1}{2} a _ {0} + \sum_ {u = 1} ^ {\infty} \left(a _ {n} \cos \frac {n \pi x}{L} + b _ {n} \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}\right)
$$

$$
\binom {a _ {n}} {b _ {n}} = \frac {1}{L} \int_ {- L} ^ {L} f (x) \binom {\cos \frac {n \pi x}{L}} {\sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}} d x, \qquad n = 0, 1, 2, \dots
$$

[註解]:

(1) 混合型邊界條件，告訴我們 $u$ 既不是一奇函數也不是一偶函數。

(2) 容易驗證所得的解是量綱平恆， $[u] = [a_n] = [b_n] = [f]$ 。

例題3.1.3.（非齊次熱傳導方程式）

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t} = u _ {x x} + g (x, t), \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = u (L, t) = 0, \qquad t > 0
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = f (x)
$$

[解]：首先由例題3.1.2知齊次解(homogeneous solution)為

$$
u _ {h} (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} t} \varphi_ {n} (x), \qquad \varphi_ {n} = \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L},
$$

其中

$$
b _ {n} = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} f (x) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x
$$

仿常微分方程理論可以考慮參數變數法(variation of parameter)，這個方法是由Lagrange所提的。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/e9565447279dd0c0c5e98dfa78d8933a9343b9dfdff2b4efbb0558f785fe4676.jpg)



圖3.1.3


令

$$
u (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} T _ {n} (t) \varphi_ {n} (x), \qquad T _ {n} (t) = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} u (x, t) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x
$$

微分並且利用原微分方程得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T _ {n} ^ {\prime} (t) = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} u _ {t} (x, t) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x \\ & \quad = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} \left[ u _ {x x} (x, t) + g (x, t) \right] \varphi_ {n} (x) d x \\ & \quad = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} u _ {x x} (x, t) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x + \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} g (x, t) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x \end{array}
$$

令

$$
g _ {n} (t) \equiv \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} g (x, t) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x
$$

由分部積分

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} u _ {x x} (x, t) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x \\ = \frac {2}{L} \Big [ u _ {x} (x, t) \varphi_ {n} (x) - u (x, t) \varphi^ {\prime} (x) \Big ] _ {0} ^ {L} + \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} u (x, t) \varphi_ {n} ^ {\prime \prime} (x) d x \\ = - \lambda_ {n} \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} u (x, t) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x = - \lambda_ {n} T _ {n} (t) \end{array}
$$

故 $T_{n}$ 满足一階非齊次微分方程

$$
T _ {n} ^ {\prime} (t) + \lambda_ {n} T _ {n} (t) = g _ {n} (t)
$$

利用參數變數法(variation of parameter)知

$$
T _ {n} (t) = e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} t} T _ {n} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} (t - \tau)} g _ {n} (\tau) d \tau
$$

其中

$$
T _ {n} (0) = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} u (x, 0) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} f (x) \varphi_ {n} (x) d x = c _ {n}
$$

代回原式解爲

$$
u (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \left[ c _ {n} e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} t} + \int_ {0} ^ {t} e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} (t - \tau)} g _ {n} (\tau) d \tau \right] \varphi_ {n} (x)
$$

[註解]:

(1) 可以藉由量綱分析驗證所得的解是否合理

$$
[ u _ {t} ] = [ u _ {x x} ] = [ g ] \quad \Longrightarrow \quad \mathrm{T} = \mathrm{L} ^ {2}, \quad [ g ] = [ u ] \mathrm{L} ^ {- 1}
$$

$$
u (x, 0) = f (x) \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ f ] = [ u ]
$$

$$
[ \varphi_ {n} ] = \left[ \sin {\frac {n \pi x}{L}} \right] = 1 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ n ] ^ {- 1} = [ x ] = [ \pi ] = [ L ] = \mathrm{L}
$$

$$
[ e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} t} ] = 1 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ \lambda_ {n} ] = [ t ] ^ {- 1} = \mathrm{L} ^ {- 2}
$$

$$
[ g _ {n} ] = [ g ], [ c _ {n} ] = [ T _ {n} (0) ] = [ f ] \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ u ] = ([ c _ {n} ] \mathrm{L} ^ {0}) \mathrm{L} ^ {0} = (\mathrm{L} ^ {0} [ g _ {n} ] \mathrm{T}) \mathrm{L} ^ {0}
$$

例题3.1.4.（漸近解）

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t} = k u _ {x x} + q (x),
$$

$$
t > 0,
$$

$$
0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = A,
$$

$$
u (L, t) = B,
$$

$$
t > 0
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = u _ {0} (x)
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/098f8737ba12b376be44fab0f0addd5f67234355ff4cf32411672b8e904e0900.jpg)



圖3.1.4


[解]: 這個問題困難處在於邊界值不等於0，但由重疊原理(線性)可以假設u可以分解為兩部份

$$
u (x, t) = v (x) + w (x, t)
$$

代回原方程式得

$$
\frac {\partial w}{\partial t} = \frac {\partial u}{\partial t} = k \frac {\partial^ {2} u}{\partial x ^ {2}} = k v ^ {\prime \prime} (x) + k \frac {\partial^ {2} w}{\partial x ^ {2}} + q (x)
$$

$$
v (0) + w (0, t) = u (0, t) = A,
$$

$$
v (L) + w (L, t) = u (L, t) = B,
$$

令 $t \to \infty$ ，並將 $w(x, t)$ 視為短暫解(transient solution: 過客也！)，所以得穩定解(stationary solution)

(D.E.) 

$$
k v ^ {\prime \prime} (x) + q (x) = 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
v (0) = A, \qquad v (L) = B, \qquad t > 0
$$

直接解此常微分方程得

$$
v (x) = \left[ B - A + \int_ {0} ^ {L} \left(\int_ {0} ^ {z} \frac {1}{k} q (s) d s\right) d z \right] \frac {x}{L} - \int_ {0} ^ {x} \left(\int_ {0} ^ {z} \frac {1}{k} q (s) d s\right) d z
$$

另外 $w$ 满足

(D.E.) 

$$
\partial_ {t} w = k \partial_ {x x} w, \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
w (0, t) = w (L, t) = 0, \quad t > 0
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
w (x, 0) = u _ {0} (x) - v (x), \qquad 0 <   x <   L,
$$

這個齊次方程式的解爲

$$
w (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} c _ {n} e ^ {- k (n \pi / L) ^ {2} t} \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}
$$

$c_{n}$ 是 $u_{0}(x)-v(x)$ 的Fourier正弦級數之係數。

[註解]:

(1) 因爲固有值 $\lambda_{n} = (n\pi / L)^{2} > 0$ ，所以

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} w (x, t) = 0, \quad \lim _ {t \to \infty} [ u (x, t) - v (x) ] = 0
$$

這也說明為何 $v(x)$ 是漸近解的理由。

## 習題 3.1

1. 試解齊次熱傳導方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
0 <   x <   \pi
$$

(B.C.) $u(0,t) = u(\pi ,t) = 0,\qquad t > 0$ 

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = \sin^3 x$ 

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \frac{3}{4} e^{-kt}\sin x - \frac{1}{4} e^{-9kt}\sin 3x$ 

2. 試解齊次熱傳導方程式

(D.E.) $u_{t} = ku_{xx},\qquad t > 0,\qquad 0 <   x <   \pi$ 

(B.C.) $u_{x}(0,t) = u_{x}(\pi ,t) = 0,\qquad t > 0$ 

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = \sin x$ 

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \frac{2}{\pi} -\frac{4}{\pi}\sum_{n = 1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{4n^{2} - 1} e^{-4n^{2}kt}\cos 2nx$ 

3. 試解齊次熱傳導方程式

(D.E.) $u_{t} - u_{xx} + au = 0,\qquad t > 0,\qquad 0 <   x <   \pi$ 

(B.C.) $u(0,t) = u_{x}(\pi ,t) = 0,\qquad t > 0$ 

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = x(\pi -x)$ 

[解答]: $u(x,t) = 8\sum_{n = 1}^{\infty}\left[\frac{4}{\pi} (2n - 1)^{-3} + (-1)^{n}(2n - 1)^{-2}\right]$ $e^{-[(2n - 1)^2 + 4a]t / 4}\sin x\left(n - \frac{1}{2}\right)x$ 

4. 試解非齊次熱傳導方程式

(D.E.) $u_{t} = ku_{xx} + A\cos \alpha t,\qquad t > 0,\qquad 0 <   x <   1$ 

(B.C.) $u_{x}(0,t) = u_{x}(1,t) = 0,\qquad t > 0$ 

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = 1 + \cos^3\pi x$ 

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \frac{3}{2} +\frac{1}{2}\cos 2\pi xe^{-4k\pi^2 t} + \frac{A}{\alpha}\sin \alpha t$ 

5. 試解非齊次熱傳導方程式

(D.E.) $u_{t} = ku_{xx} + Q,\qquad t > 0,\qquad 0 <   x <   L$ 

(B.C.) $u(0,t) = A,\quad u(L,t) = B,\qquad t > 0$ 

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = 0,\qquad 0 <   x <   L$ 

[解答]： $u(x,t) = -\frac{Qx^2}{2k} +\left[\frac{B - A}{L} +\frac{QL}{2k}\right]x + A + \sum_{n = 1}^{\infty}b_ne^{-\frac{n^2\pi^2}{L^2} kt}\sin \frac{n\pi x}{L}$ $b_{n} = \frac{2QL^{2}}{kn^{3}\pi^{3}} [(-1)^{n} - 1] + \frac{2B(01)^{n}}{n\pi} -\frac{2A}{n\pi}$ 

6. 試解次熱傳導方程式

(D.E.) $u_{t} = ku_{xx} - \nu (u - u_{0}),\qquad t > 0,\qquad 0 <   x <   L$ 

(B.C.) $u(0,t) = \phi_1(t),\quad u(L,t) = \phi_2(t),\qquad t > 0$ 

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = f(x),\qquad 0 <   x <   L$ 

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \sum_{n = 1}^{\infty}a_n e^{-(k\frac{n^2\pi^2}{L^2} +\nu)t}\sin \frac{n\pi x}{L}$ 

$$
a _ {n} = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} f (x) \sin {\frac {n \pi x}{L}} d x + \frac {2 k n \pi}{L ^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {t} [ \phi_ {1} (\tau) - (- 1) ^ {n} \phi_ {2} (\tau) ] e ^ {(\nu + k \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2}}{L ^ {2}}) \tau} d \tau
$$

7. 試解非齊次熱傳導方程式並求其漸近解

(D.E.) $u_{t} = ku_{xx} + x,\qquad t > 0,\qquad 0 <   x <   L$ 

(B.C.) $u_{x}(0,t) = u_{x}(L,t) = 0,\qquad t > 0$ 

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = f(x),\qquad 0 <   x <   L$ 

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \frac{b_0 + Lt}{2} +\sum_{n = 1}^{\infty}\frac{2}{kL\lambda_n^2} [(-1)^n -1]\cos \frac{n\pi x}{L}$ 

$$
+ \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \left(b _ {n} - \frac {2}{k L \lambda_ {n} ^ {2}} [ (- 1) ^ {n} - 1 ]\right) e ^ {- \lambda_ {n} k t} \cos \frac {n \pi x}{L}
$$

$$
\lambda_ {n} = \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2}}{L ^ {2}}, \quad b _ {n} = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} f (x) \cos \frac {n \pi x}{L} d x
$$

8. (Duhamel原理) 已知 $v(x, t, s)$ 滿足齊次熱傳導方程式

$$
v _ {t} = v _ {x x}, \qquad t \geq s, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) $v(0,t,s) = v(L,t,s) = 0,\qquad t\geq s$ 

(I.C.) $v(x,s,s) = q(x,s),\qquad 0\leq x\leq L$ 

證明

$$
u (x, t) = \int_ {0} ^ {t} v (x, t, s) d s
$$

滿足非齊次熱傳導方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t} = u _ {x x} + q (x, t), \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) $u(0,t) = u(L,t) = 0,\qquad t\geq 0$ 

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = 0,\qquad 0\leq x\leq L$ 

## § 3.2 波動方程式

波動方程是形式如

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} u}{\partial t ^ {2}} - c ^ {2} \Delta u = f (x, t)
$$

的方程， $u(x,t)$ 是時間變量t，及空間變量 $x=(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})$ ，兩個變數的函數，Laplace算子 $\Delta$ 僅作用於空間變量。這個方程刻劃了許多物理過程的許多簡單現象，例如彈性介質之振動，在一維情形(n=1)，這個方程刻劃弦振動的縱向振動。在二維(n=2)，則是薄膜或鼓皮之振動，三維空間(n=3)，則波動方程描述的是聲波或電磁波之傳遞。在這個方程 $f(x,t)$ 代表外力，而且容易看出來常數c具有 $[x]/[t]$ 之量綱，它實際上是在給定的具體問題中波的傳播速度。 $\frac{\partial^{2}u}{\partial t^{2}}$ 表示弦上任一點的加速度，所以波動方程是牛頓力學的直接推論；琴弦每一小段的加速度，都與這一小段所受的拉力成正比。波動方程是雙曲型方程的典型代表。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/05b09e567a8e7567a0366c8b2e54f71df2d4f83421792f12d5bdc9fd40dceaad.jpg)



圖3.2.1


例題3.2.1.（齊次波動方程式）

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} = c ^ {2} u _ {x x}, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = u (L, t) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = f (x), \qquad u _ {t} (x, 0) = g (x), \qquad - L \leq x \leq L
$$

[解]: 設 $u(x,t) = T(t)\varphi (x)$ ，直接微分後代入原方程式得

$$
T ^ {\prime \prime} (t) + c ^ {2} \lambda T (t) = 0, \qquad \left\{ \begin{array}{l} \varphi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + \lambda \varphi (x) = 0 \\ \varphi (0) = \varphi (L) = 0 \end{array} \right.
$$

$\varphi$ 滿足二階常微分方程，由Sturm-Liouville之理論知固有值與固有函數爲

$$
\lambda_ {n} = (\frac {n \pi}{L}) ^ {2}, \qquad \varphi_ {n} (x) = \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}, \qquad n = 1, 2, 3, \dots
$$

而且

$$
T _ {n} (t) = a _ {n} \cos {\frac {n \pi c t}{L}} + b _ {n} \sin {\frac {n \pi c t}{L}}
$$

其中係數 $a_{n}$ 、 $b_{n}$ 為待求。原波動方程式之解可表為

$$
u (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} T _ {n} (t) \varphi_ {n} (x) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \left(a _ {n} \cos \frac {n \pi c t}{L} + b _ {n} \sin \frac {n \pi c t}{L}\right) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}
$$

對t微分得

$$
u _ {t} (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {n \pi c}{L} \bigg (- a _ {n} \sin \frac {n \pi c t}{L} + b _ {n} \cos \frac {n \pi c t}{L} \bigg) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}
$$

現在將初始值(initial value)代入得

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} u (x, 0) = f (x) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} a _ {n} \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} \\ u _ {t} (x, 0) = g (x) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {n \pi c}{L} b _ {n} \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} \end{array} \right.
$$

即 f 、 g 可表為 Fourier 正弦級數，因此係數為

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} a _ {n} = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} f (x) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} d x, \\ b _ {n} = \frac {2}{n \pi c} \int_ {0} ^ {L} g (x) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} d x \end{array} \right.
$$

[註解]:

(1) 邊界條件 $u(0, t) = u(L, t) = 0$ 告訴我們 $u$ 必然是一奇函數。按弦振動理論，如果將因一根繩子一端固定並上下振動產生一個波，當波前進至固定端點後反射回來的波形必然是原波形對 $x$ -軸的倒影，也就是說滿足這種邊界條件的波是一個奇函數。同理邊界條件 $u_x(0, t) = 0$ 則函數在原點附近是左右對稱因此 $u$ 必然是一偶函數。

(2) 可以藉由量綱分析驗證所得的解是否合理

$$
[ u _ {t t} ] = [ c ^ {2} u _ {x x} ] \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ c ] = \frac {\mathrm{L}}{\mathrm{T}} (\text {速度})
$$

$$
u (x, 0) = f (x), \quad u _ {t} (x, 0) = g (x) \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ f ] = [ u ], \quad [ g ] = [ u ] / \mathrm{T}
$$

$$
[ \varphi_ {n} ] = \left[ \sin {\frac {n \pi x}{L}} \right] = 1 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad [ n ] ^ {- 1} = [ x ] = [ \pi ] = [ L ] = L
$$

$\left[\cos n\pi ct\right] = \left[\sin n\pi ct\right] = 1\quad \Longrightarrow \quad \left[c\right] = \frac{\mathrm{L}}{\mathrm{T}}$ （時空是藉由 $c$ 聯結）

$$
[ a _ {n} ] = \frac {1}{\mathrm{L}} [ f ] \mathrm{L} ^ {0} \mathrm{L} = [ f ] = [ u ], \qquad [ b _ {n} ] = \frac {1}{[ n \pi c ]} [ g ] \mathrm{L} ^ {0} \mathrm{L} = \mathrm{T} [ g ] = [ f ] = [ u ]
$$

這個解(Fourier級數解)實際上就是D. Bernoulli所得的解。但是，d'Alembert卻是由另一個角度得到弦振動方程的解，也因此引起兩方之論戰。但基本上D. Bernoulli與d'Alembert的解是等價的。

例題3.2.2. (d'Alembert公式) 證明例題3.2.1的解可以表示爲

$$
u (x, t) = \frac {1}{2} [ f (x - c t) + f (x + c t) ] + \frac {1}{2 c} \int_ {x - c t} ^ {x + c t} g (s) d s
$$

[解]: 這只是和差化積或積化和差的簡單應用

$$
\begin{array}{r} \cos \alpha \sin \beta = \frac {1}{2} [ \sin (\alpha + \beta) - \sin (\alpha - \beta) ] \\ \sin \alpha \sin \beta = \frac {1}{2} [ \cos (\alpha - \beta) - \cos (\alpha - \beta) ] \end{array}
$$

我們將 $u(x,t)$ 分解為 $u_{1}(x,t)$ 與 $u_{2}(x,t)$ 兩部份

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {u _ {1} (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} a _ {n} \cos \frac {n \pi c t}{L} \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{2} \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} a _ {n} \bigg (\sin \frac {n \pi}{L} (x + c t) + \sin \frac {n \pi}{L} (x - c t) \bigg)} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{2} [ f (x + c t) + f (x - c t) ]} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {\partial}{\partial t} u _ {2} (x, t) = \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} \sin \frac {n \pi c t}{L} \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {b _ {n}}{2} \bigg (\cos \frac {n \pi}{L} (x - c t) - \cos \frac {n \pi x}{L} (x + c t) \bigg)} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{2} \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {n \pi c}{L} b _ {n} \bigg (\sin \frac {n \pi}{L} (x + c t) + \sin \frac {n \pi}{L} (x - c t) \bigg)} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{2} [ g (x + c t) + g (x - c t) ]} \end{array}
$$

積分得

$$
u _ {2} (x, t) = \frac {1}{2 c} \int_ {x - c t} ^ {x + c t} g (s) d s
$$

[註解]:

(1) 因爲 $[f] = [u]$ 、 $[g] = [u]T^{-1}$ 、 $[c] = L / T$ ，所以容易驗證d'Alembert公式是量綱平衡

$$
[ u ] = [ f ] = \frac {1}{[ c ]} [ g ] \mathrm{L}
$$

(2) 直接可以驗證波動方程式有兩個以下形式的特解：

$$
\phi_ {1} (x - c t), \qquad \phi_ {2} (x + c t)
$$

其中 $\phi_{1}$ 、 $\phi_{2}$ 是任意二階可微的函數， $\phi_{1}(x - ct)$ 可以解釋為弦沿著 $x-$ 軸右方以速度 $c$ 前進的波。 $\phi_{2}(x + ct)$ 則是以相同速度，但朝反方向前進。原先，函數 $u$ 之變數 $x$ 、 $t$ 是分開，但如今則是藉由 $c$ 來連結。所以， $\phi_{1}$ 、 $\phi_{2}$ 滿足的常微分方程。我們稱這種波為行波解（traveling wave solution）。順便一提的是 $[x - ct] = [x + ct] = [x]$ ！

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/385d17639d04c489e00cc5d9e71d846a9bb4f67ca948bf89669218e52cd05ae7.jpg)



圖 3.2.2


例題3.2.3. (自由邊界: free ends)

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} = c ^ {2} u _ {x x}, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u _ {x} (0, t) = u _ {x} (L, t) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = f (x), \qquad u _ {t} (x, 0) = g (x), \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L
$$

[解]：由邊界條件 $u_{x}(0,t)=u_{x}(L,t)=0$ ，可推測 $u(x,t)$ 可以表為Fourier餘弦級數，首先將 $f(x)$ 、 $g(x)$ 表為Fourier餘弦級數

$$
f (x) = \frac {1}{2} a _ {0} + \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} a _ {n} \cos \frac {n \pi x}{L}, \quad g (x) = \frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {0} + \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \alpha_ {n} \cos \frac {n \pi x}{L}
$$

所以波動方程式的解可以表示爲

$$
u (x, t) = \frac {1}{2} A _ {0} (t) + \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} A _ {n} (t) \cos \frac {n \pi x}{L}
$$

其中係數 $A_{n}$ 滿足二階微分方程

$$
A _ {n} ^ {\prime \prime} (t) + \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} c ^ {2}}{L ^ {2}} A _ {n} (t) = 0, \qquad A _ {n} (0) = a _ {n}, \qquad A _ {n} ^ {\prime} (0) = \alpha_ {n}
$$

二階微分方程的解爲

$$
A _ {n} (t) = a _ {n} \cos \frac {n \pi c t}{L} + \frac {L \alpha_ {n}}{n \pi c} \sin \frac {n \pi c t}{L}, \qquad n > 0
$$

$$
A _ {0} (t) = a _ {0} + \alpha_ {0} t
$$

所以

$$
u (x, t) = \frac {1}{2} (a _ {0} + \alpha_ {0} t) + \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \cos \frac {n \pi x}{L} \bigg (a _ {n} \cos \frac {n \pi c t}{L} + \frac {L \alpha_ {n}}{n \pi c} \sin \frac {n \pi c t}{L} \bigg)
$$

[註解]:

(1) 量綱分析得 $[u] = [f] = [g]\mathrm{T} 、 [c] = \mathrm{LT}^{-1} 、 [a_n] = [f] 、 [\alpha_n] = [g]$ ，因此所得的解是量綱平恆

$$
[ u ] = [ a _ {0} ] = [ \alpha_ {0} t ] = [ a _ {n} ] = [ L \alpha_ {n} / n \pi c ]
$$

例題3.2.4.(帶阻尼之波動方程)

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} + 2 a u _ {t} = c ^ {2} u _ {x x}, \qquad 0 <   x <   \pi , \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u _ {x} (0, t) = u _ {x} (\pi , t) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = f (x), \qquad u _ {t} (x, 0) = 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   \pi
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/96b6cc6689f96b698f312d6fc5c8800c14a517ddb9cea410a8ea7e6ad19dda1c.jpg)



圖3.2.3


[解]: 假設 $u(x,t)=\varphi(x)T(t)$ ，則

$$
\frac {T ^ {\prime \prime} (t) + 2 a T ^ {\prime} (t)}{c ^ {2} T (t)} = \frac {\varphi^ {\prime \prime} (x)}{\varphi (x)} \equiv - \lambda
$$

所以 $\varphi$ 滿足邊界值問題

$$
\varphi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + \lambda \varphi (x) = 0, \qquad \varphi (0) = \varphi (\pi) = 0
$$

仿前面之計算可得固有值與固有函數

$$
\lambda_ {n} = n ^ {2}, \quad \varphi_ {n} (x) = \sin n x, \qquad n = 1, 2, 3, \dots
$$

此時對應的 $T_{n}$ 滿足初始值問題

$$
T _ {n} ^ {\prime \prime} + 2 a T _ {n} ^ {\prime} + n ^ {2} c ^ {2} T _ {n} = 0, \qquad T _ {n} (0) = 1, \quad T _ {n} ^ {\prime} (0) = 0
$$

這個二階微分方程的解可以表示為(利用積分因子)

$$
T _ {n} (t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} e ^ {- a t} \bigg (\cosh \sqrt {a ^ {2} - n ^ {2} c ^ {2}} t + \frac {a}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} - n ^ {2} c ^ {2}}} \sinh \sqrt {a ^ {2} - n ^ {2} c ^ {2}} t \bigg), & n <   \frac {a}{c} \\ e ^ {- a t} (1 + a t), \qquad n = \frac {a}{c} \\ e ^ {- a t} \bigg (\cos \sqrt {n ^ {2} c ^ {2} - a ^ {2}} t + \frac {a}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} - n ^ {2} c ^ {2}}} \sin \sqrt {n ^ {2} c ^ {2} - a ^ {2}} t \bigg), & n > \frac {a}{c} \end{array} \right.
$$

因此 u 之形式解 (formal solution) 爲

$$
u (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} T _ {n} (t) \sin n x
$$

其中係數 $b_{n}$ 由初始值所決定

$$
u (x, 0) = f (x) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} \sin n x
$$

意即 $b_{n}$ 是 $f(x)$ 級數之Fourier係數

$$
b _ {n} = \frac {2}{\pi} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} f (x) \sin n x d x, \qquad n = 1, 2, 3, \dots
$$

[註解]:

(1) 量綱分析得 $[a] = \mathrm{T}^{-1}$ ，因此所得的解是量綱平恆。

例題3.2.5.（非齊次波動方程）

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} - c ^ {2} u _ {x x} = f (x, t), \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = u (L, t) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = \phi_ {0} (x), \qquad u _ {t} (x, 0) = \phi_ {1} (x), \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/eb82354c2a0b3ffd02f8617aeec63b8974be746c2838ebaabe98074ce371b613.jpg)



圖3.2.4


[解]: 我們可以把解分解成兩部份(利用線性)

$$
u (x, t) = v (x, t) + w (x, t)
$$

其中 v 滿足非齊次微分方程但邊界值與初始值都為 0，而 w 則滿足齊次微分方程，但保留初始值

(D.E.) 

$$
v _ {t t} - c ^ {2} v _ {x x} = f (x, t), \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
v (0, t) = v (L, t) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
v (x, 0) = v _ {t} (x, 0) = \mathbf {0}, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L
$$

(D.E.) 

$$
w _ {t t} - c ^ {2} w _ {x x} = 0, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
w _ {x} (0, t) = w _ {x} (\pi , t) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
w (x, 0) = \phi_ {0} (x), \quad w _ {t} (x, 0) = \phi_ {1} (x), \quad 0 \leq x \leq L
$$

由參數變數法，可以假設 $v(x,t)$ 可以表示爲

$$
v (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} T _ {n} (t) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}
$$

$v(x,t)$ 自動滿足邊界條件 $v(0,t) = v(L,t) = 0$ （因爲固有函數爲 $\varphi_{n}(x) = \sin \frac{n\pi x}{L}$ ），將 $v$ 代回非齊次微分方程得

$$
\sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \left(T _ {n} ^ {\prime \prime} (t) + \omega_ {n} ^ {2} T _ {n} (t)\right) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} = f (x, t), \qquad \omega_ {n} = \frac {n \pi c}{L}
$$

我們將 $f(x,t)$ 展成Fourier級數

$$
f (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} f _ {n} (t) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}, \qquad f _ {n} (t) = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} f (x, t) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} d x
$$

因此 $T_{n}$ 滿足非齊次常微分方程

$$
T _ {n} ^ {\prime \prime} (t) + \omega_ {n} ^ {2} T _ {n} (t) = f _ {n} (t), \qquad n = 1, 2, 3, \dots
$$

因爲 $v(x,0)=v_{t}(x,0)=0$ ，因此 $T_{n}(0)=T_{n}^{\prime}(0)=0$ ，所以由非齊次微分方程的理論可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T _ {n} (t) = \frac {1}{\omega_ {n}} \int_ {0} ^ {L} f _ {n} (\tau) \sin \omega_ {n} (t - \tau) d \tau \\ & \qquad = \frac {2}{L \omega_ {n}} \int_ {0} ^ {L} \sin \omega_ {n} (t - \tau) d \tau \int_ {0} ^ {L} f (x, \tau) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} d x \end{array}
$$

整理可得

$$
u (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} T _ {n} (t) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} + \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \left(a _ {n} \cos \frac {n \pi c t}{L} + b _ {n} \sin \frac {n \pi c t}{L}\right) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L}
$$

其中係數 $a_{n}$ 與 $b_{n}$ 為

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} a _ {n} = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} \phi_ {0} (x) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} d x \\ b _ {n} = \frac {2}{n \pi c} \int_ {0} ^ {L} \phi_ {1} (x) \sin \frac {n \pi x}{L} d x \end{array} \right.
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/ff3cd04003b812bab6ff0daa0c3dd1e3e1fb59f772383ab1990867b616961895.jpg)



圖 3.2.5


例题3.2.6.（非齊次初始-邊界值波動方程）

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} - c ^ {2} u _ {x x} = f (x, t), \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L, \qquad 0 \leq t,\tag{B.C.}
$$

$$
u (0, t) = \psi_ {0} (t), \qquad u (L, t) = \psi_ {1} (t), \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = \phi_ {0} (x), \qquad u _ {t} (x, 0) = \phi_ {1} (x), \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L
$$

[解]: 我們仍然可以利用線性將 u 分解成兩部份 (利用線性)、 $u(x,t)=v(x,t)+w(x,t)$ ，首先要把邊界條件化為 0。利用分點公式可以考慮

$$
w (x, t) = \psi_ {0} (t) + (\psi_ {1} (t) - \psi_ {0} (t)) \frac {x}{L}
$$

顯然 w 滿足邊界條件 $w(0,t)=\psi_{0}(t)$ 、 $w(L,t)=\psi_{1}(t)$ ，所以對 v 而言其邊界邊必然等於 0

$$
v (0, t) = v (L, t) = 0, \qquad t > 0
$$

而初始條件則爲

$$
v (x, 0) = u (x, 0) - w (x, 0) = \phi_ {0} (x) - \psi_ {0} (0) - (\psi_ {1} (0) - \psi_ {0} (0)) \frac {x}{L} = \bar {\phi} _ {0} (x)
$$

$$
v _ {t} (x, 0) = u _ {t} (x, 0) - w _ {t} (x, 0) = \phi_ {1} (x) - \psi_ {0} ^ {\prime} (0) - (\psi_ {1} ^ {\prime} (0) - \psi_ {0} ^ {\prime} (0)) \frac {x}{\overline {{L}}} = \bar {\phi} _ {1} (x)
$$

而且 $v$ 滿足非齊次方程

$$
v _ {t t} - c ^ {2} v _ {x x} = \bar {f} (x, t) \equiv f (x, t) - \psi_ {0} ^ {\prime \prime} (t) - [ \psi_ {1} ^ {\prime \prime} (t) - \psi_ {0} ^ {\prime \prime} (t) ] \frac {x}{L}
$$

此時可以利用前面的方法解v，細節我們就省略。

例題3.2.7.（圓盤區域之波動方程：Bessel函數）

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} - c ^ {2} (u _ {r r} + \frac {1}{r} u _ {r}) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq r \leq 1, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (1, t) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (r, 0) = f (r), \qquad u _ {t} (r, 0) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq r \leq 1
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/c873c3556f5110ae990603f7b7ac9cfc126f3f36293024dab3c44cea206ec612.jpg)



圖3.2.6


[解]: 由分離變數法，假設 $u(r,t) = R(r)T(t)$ ，則原波動方程成為兩組常微分方程

$$
r ^ {2} R ^ {\prime \prime} + r R ^ {\prime} + \lambda^ {2} r ^ {2} R = 0
$$

$$
T ^ {\prime \prime} + \lambda^ {2} c ^ {2} T = 0
$$

$T$ 部份容易解

$$
T (t) = k _ {1} \sin \lambda c t + k _ {2} \cos \lambda c t, \qquad k _ {1}, k _ {2} \in \mathbb {R}
$$

關於 $R$ 部份，我們做一下變數變換，令 $\xi = \lambda r$ ，則

$$
\xi^ {2} R ^ {\prime \prime} (\xi) + \xi R ^ {\prime} (\xi) + \xi^ {2} R (\xi) = 0
$$

這是零階Bessel方程，所以由Bessel方程的理論可得

$$
R (\xi) = c _ {1} J _ {0} (\xi) + c _ {2} Y _ {0} (\xi)
$$

因爲 $Y_0(\lambda r) \to -\infty$ ，當 $r \to 0$ ，因此由邊界條件可得 $c_2 = 0$ ，故 $J_0(\lambda) = 0$ 。所以固有值正是Bessel函數 $J_0(\lambda)$ 的根，但 $J_0(\lambda)$ 有無窮多個離散的根，設爲 $\lambda_1, \lambda_2, \lambda_3, \cdots$ ，則對應的固有函數爲 $J_0(\lambda_n r)$ ，因此

$$
u _ {n} (r, t) = J _ {0} (\lambda_ {n} r) \sin \lambda_ {n} c t, v _ {n} (r, t) = J _ {0} (\lambda_ {n} r) \cos \lambda_ {n} c t, n = 1, 2, 3, \dots
$$

所以波動方程的解爲

$$
\begin{array}{r} u (r, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} [ a _ {n} u _ {n} (r, t) + b _ {n} v _ {n} (r, t) ] \\ = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} [ a _ {n} J _ {0} (\lambda_ {n} r) \sin \lambda_ {n} c t + b _ {n} J _ {0} (\lambda_ {n} t) \cos \lambda_ {n} c t ] \end{array}
$$

此時初始值爲

$$
u (r, 0) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} J _ {0} (\lambda_ {n} r) = f (r), \quad u _ {t} (r, 0) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \lambda_ {n} c a _ {n} J _ {0} (\lambda_ {n} r) = 0
$$

利用 Bessel 函數之展開式可得係數 $a_{n}$ 、 $b_{n}$ 

$$
a _ {n} = 0, \qquad b _ {n} = \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {1} f (r) J _ {0} (\lambda_ {n} r) d r}{\int_ {0} ^ {1} r [ J _ {0} (\lambda_ {n} r) ] ^ {2} d r}, \qquad n = 1, 2, \dots
$$

所以原波動方程的解爲

$$
u (r, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} b _ {n} J _ {0} (\lambda_ {n} r) \cos \lambda_ {n} c t
$$

## 習題3.2

1. 試解齊次波動方程式

(D.E.) $u_{tt} = c^2 u_{xx},\qquad t > 0,\qquad 0 <   x <   L$ 

(B.C.) $u(0,t) = u(L,t) = 0,\qquad t > 0$ 

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = f(x),\qquad u_{t}(x,0) = 0,\qquad 0\leq x\leq L$ 

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \sum_{n = 1}^{\infty}a_n\cos {\frac{n\pi ct}{L}}\sin {\frac{n\pi x}{L}},\quad a_n = \frac{2}{L}\int_0^L f(x)\sin {\frac{n\pi x}{L}} dx$ 

2. 試解齊次波動方程式

$$
\mathrm{(D.E.)} \qquad u _ {t t} = c ^ {2} u _ {x x}, \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) $u(0,t) = u(L,t) = 0,\qquad t > 0$ 

$$
u (x, 0) = 1 0 \sin {\frac {\pi x}{L}}, \qquad u _ {t} (x, 0) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L
$$

[解答]: $u(x,t) = 10\cos \frac{\pi ct}{L}\sin \frac{\pi x}{L}$ 

3. 試解齊次波動方程式

$$
u _ {t t} = u _ {x x}, \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   1
$$

$$
u (0, t) = u (1, t) = 0, \quad t > 0 \tag {B.C.}
$$

$$
u (x, 0) = x (1 - x), \quad u _ {t} (x, 0) = \sin 7 x, \quad 0 \leq x \leq 1 \tag {I.C.}
$$

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \frac{1}{7\pi}\sin 7\pi t\sin 7\pi x$ $+ \sum_{n = 1}^{\infty}\frac{8}{((2n + 1)\pi)^3}\cos (2n + 1)\pi t\sin (2n + 1)\pi x$ 

4. 試解齊次波動方程式

## 3.2 波動方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} = c ^ {2} u _ {x x}, \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = u (L, t) = 0, \qquad t > 0
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = 0, \qquad u _ {t} (x, 0) = \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} 0, & 0 \leq x \leq \frac {L}{4} \\ - V, & \frac {L}{4} \leq x \leq \frac {L}{2} \\ 0, & \frac {L}{2} \leq x \leq L \end{array} \right.
$$

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \sum_{n = 1}^{\infty}b_n\frac{\sin\frac{n\pi ct}{L}}{\frac{n\pi c}{L}}\sin \frac{n\pi x}{L}$ 

$$
b _ {n} = \frac {2}{L} \int_ {0} ^ {L} g (x) \sin {\frac {n \pi x}{L}} d x = \frac {2 V}{n \pi} (\cos {\frac {n \pi}{2}} - \cos {\frac {n \pi}{4}})
$$

## 5. 試解波動方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} = c ^ {2} u _ {x x}, \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = 0, \qquad u _ {x} (L, t) = \frac {Q}{E}, \qquad t > 0
$$

$$
\text {(I.C.)} \quad u (x, 0) = u _ {t} (x, 0) = 0, \quad 0 \leq x \leq L
$$

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \frac{Q}{E} x - \frac{8QL}{\pi^2E}\sum_{n = 0}^{\infty}(-1)^n\frac{\cos\frac{(2n + 1)\pi at}{2L}\sin\frac{(2n + 1)\pi x}{2L}}{(2n + 1)^2}$ 

## 6. 試解齊次波動方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} = c ^ {2} u _ {x x}, \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = 0, \qquad u _ {x} (L, t) = \frac {A}{E} \sin w t, \qquad t > 0
$$

$$
\text {(I.C.)} \quad u (x, 0) = u _ {t} (x, 0) = 0, \quad 0 \leq x \leq L
$$

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \frac{cA}{Ew}\frac{\sin\frac{wx}{c}\sin wt}{\cos\frac{wL}{c}} +$ $\frac{2cwA}{EL}\sum_{n = 0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{n - 1}}{k_n}\sin \frac{k_nx}{w^2 - k_n^2c^2}\sin ck_nt$ $k_{n} = \frac{(2n + 1)\pi}{2L},w\neq ak_n$ 

7. 試解電報方程式

(D.E.) 

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = u (\pi , t) = 0, \qquad t > 0
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = \sin^ {3} {\frac {1}{2}} x, \qquad u _ {t} (x, 0) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq \pi
$$

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \frac{1}{4} e^{-\frac{t}{2}}\left[3(1 + \frac{t}{2})\sin \frac{x}{2} -(\cos \sqrt{2} t + \frac{\sqrt{2}}{4}\sin \sqrt{2} t)\sin \frac{3x}{2}\right]$ 

## 8. 試解非齊次波動方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} = c ^ {2} u _ {x x} + b x (x - L), \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   L
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = u (L, t) = 0, \qquad t > 0
$$

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = u_{t}(x,0) = 0,\qquad 0\leq x\leq L$ 

[解答]: $u(x,t) = -\frac{bx}{12} (x^3 -2x^2 L + L^3)$ $+ \frac{8L^4}{\pi^5}\sum_{n = 0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(2n + 1)^5}\cos \frac{(2n + 1)\pi ct}{L}\sin \frac{(2n + 1)\pi x}{L}$ 

9. 試解非齊次波動方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {t t} = u _ {x x} + t x, \qquad t > 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   \pi
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (0, t) = u (\pi , t) = 0, \qquad t > 0
$$

(I.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = \sin x, \qquad u _ {t} (x, 0) = 5 \sin 2 x - 3 \sin 5 x, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq L
$$

[解答]： $u(x,t) = 5 + \frac{5}{\pi} x - \frac{30}{\pi} e^{-2t}\sin x + \frac{5}{\pi} e^{-8t}\sin 2x + (1 + \frac{10}{\pi})e^{-18t}\sin 3x$ $\frac{5}{2\pi} e^{-32t}\sin 4x - (1 + \frac{6}{\pi})e^{-50t}\sin 5x + \sum_{n = 0}^{\infty}\frac{10}{n\pi}[2(-1)^n -1]e^{-2n^2 t}\sin nx$ 

## 10. 試解非齊次波動方程式

(D.E.) $u_{tt} = u_{xx} + \cos 2\pi x\cos 2\pi t,\qquad t > 0,\qquad 0 <   x <   1$ 

(B.C.) $u_{x}(0,t) = u_{x}(1,t) = 0,\qquad t > 0$ 

(I.C.) $u(x,0) = \cos^2\pi x,\qquad u_t(x,0) = 2\cos 2\pi x,\qquad 0\leq x\leq L$ 

[解答]: $u(x,t) = \frac{1}{2} +\left(\frac{1}{2}\cos 2\pi t + \frac{t}{4\pi}\sin 2\pi t\right)\cos 2\pi x$ 

## 11. 試解圓盤區域之波動方程

$$
u _ {t t} + 2 h u _ {t} = c ^ {2} (u _ {r r} + \frac {1}{r} u _ {r}), \qquad 0 \leq r \leq R, \qquad 0 \leq t,
$$

(B.C.) $u(R,t) = 0,\qquad 0\leq t,$ 

(I.C.) $u(r,0) = f(r),\qquad u_t(r,0) = 0,\qquad 0\leq r\leq 1$ 

[解答]: $u(r,t)=\frac{2}{R^{2}}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}e^{-ht}\left(\cos q_{n}t+\frac{h}{q_{n}}\sin q_{n}t\right)$ $\frac{J_{0}(\mu_{n}r/R)}{J_{1}^{2}(\mu_{n})}\int_{0}^{R}\rho f(\rho)J_{0}(\mu_{n}\rho/R)d\rho$ $q_{n}=\sqrt{\frac{c^{2}\mu_{n}^{2}}{R^{2}}-h^{2}},\mu_{1},\mu_{2},\cdots$ 是 $J_{0}(\mu)$ 之正根。

## § 3.3 Laplace 方程式

Laplace方程

$$
\Delta u = \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \frac {\partial^ {2} u}{\partial x _ {j} ^ {2}} = 0
$$

是數學物理與偏微分方程理論中最重要的方程之一。幾乎所有的物理與幾何都會出現的方程。Laplace方程是橢圓型方程最簡單的代表。在二維空間Laplace方程解的性質與解析函數的性質相似，其實我們可以說研究解析函數為等價於Laplace方程（即調和函數）。雖然對於Laplace方程之研究開始於18世紀(Laplace,1782)，但其研究一直持續到現代，並且有一系列有價值的未解問題與Laplace方程有關。

例題3.3.1.（長方形區域）Laplace方程的邊界值問題

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   a, \qquad 0 <   y <   b\tag{B.C.}
$$

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} u (x, 0) = g (x), & u (x, b) = h (x), \qquad 0 <   x <   a, \\ u (0, y) = 0, & u (a, y) = 0, \qquad 0 <   y <   b \end{array} \right.
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/08e7b5bb68ec8020f295835319478f92e9899f5ce6e793ae9e9dd96526cfb3ec.jpg)



圖3.3.1


[解]：由分離變數法，設 $u(x,y)=\varphi(x)\psi(y)$ ，則 $\varphi$ 、 $\psi$ 滿足常微分方程

$$
\varphi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + \lambda \varphi (x) = 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   a, \qquad \varphi (0) = \varphi (a) = 0,
$$

$$
\psi^ {\prime \prime} (y) - \lambda \psi (y) = 0, \qquad 0 <   y <   b
$$

首先解 $\varphi$ 得固有值、固有函数為

$$
\lambda_ {m} = \left(\frac {m \pi}{a}\right) ^ {2}, \qquad \varphi_ {m} (x) = \sin \frac {m \pi x}{a}, \qquad m = 1, 2, \dots
$$

顯然可設 $\psi_{m}$ 為微分方程 $\psi_{m}^{\prime \prime} - \lambda_{m}\psi_{m} = 0$ 之解，則

$$
u _ {m} (x, y) = \varphi_ {m} (x) \psi_ {m} (y) = \sin {\frac {m \pi x}{a}} \psi_ {m} (y)
$$

$$
u _ {m} (0, y) = u _ {m} (a, y) = 0
$$

因此由重疊原理知 $u(x,y)$ 可表爲

$$
u (x, y) = \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {\infty} \sin \frac {m \pi x}{a} \psi_ {m} (y)
$$

$\psi_{m}(y)$ 可由邊界值來決定

$$
u (x, 0) = g (x) = \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {\infty} \psi_ {m} (0) \sin \frac {m \pi x}{a},
$$

$$
u (x, b) = h (x) = \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {\infty} \psi_ {m} (b) \sin \frac {m \pi x}{a}, \qquad 0 <   x <   a
$$

換句話說g、h可表為Fourier正弦級數，其係數為

$$
\psi_ {m} (0) = g _ {m} = \frac {2}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} g (x) \sin {\frac {m \pi x}{a}} d x,
$$

$$
\psi_ {m} (h) = h _ {m} = \frac {2}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} h (x) \sin {\frac {m \pi x}{a}} d x,
$$

因此 $\psi_{m}(y)$ 滿足常微分方程的邊界值問題

$$
\psi_ {m} ^ {\prime \prime} - \lambda_ {m} \psi_ {m} (y) = 0, \qquad \psi_ {m} (0) = g _ {m}, \qquad \psi_ {m} (b) = h _ {m},
$$

其解(形式解)爲

$$
u (x, y) = \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {\infty} g _ {m} \frac {\sinh \frac {m \pi (b - y)}{a}}{\sinh \frac {m \pi b}{a}} \sin \frac {m \pi x}{a} + \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {\infty} h _ {m} \frac {\sinh \frac {m \pi y}{a}}{\sinh \frac {m \pi b}{a}} \sin \frac {m \pi x}{a}
$$

例題3.3.2.（長方形區域）Laplace方程的邊界值問題

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   a, \qquad 0 <   y <   b\tag{B.C.}
$$

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} u (x, 0) = u (x, b) = 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   a, \\ u (0, y) = f (y), \qquad u (a, y) = k (y), \qquad 0 <   y <   b \end{array} \right.
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/e86a2ae2ce25f12117794c838bb193c701177c2476be3fb949786301c8e1e5fb.jpg)



圖3.3.2


[解]: 仿照上一題之方法，可得

$$
u (x, y) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} f _ {n} \frac {\sinh \frac {n \pi (a - x)}{b}}{\sinh \frac {n \pi a}{b}} \sin \frac {n \pi y}{b} + \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} k _ {n} \frac {\sinh \frac {n \pi x}{b}}{\sinh \frac {n \pi a}{b}} \sin \frac {n \pi y}{b}
$$

其中

$$
f _ {n} = \frac {2}{b} \int_ {0} ^ {b} f (y) \sin {\frac {n \pi y}{b}} d y
$$

$$
k _ {n} = \frac {2}{b} \int_ {0} ^ {b} k (y) \sin {\frac {n \pi y}{b}} d y
$$

例題3.3.3.（正方形區域）Laplace方程的邊界值問題

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = 0, \qquad 0 <   x, y <   1,
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (x, 0) = u (0, y) = u (1, y) = 0, \quad u (x, 1) = u _ {1} (x),
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/5af9437d2e92adac6898d42befcd3252c3e85f89db39af4f4fab110c9a6a2cdb.jpg)



圖 3.3.3


[解]: 假設 $u(x,y)=X(x)Y(y)$ ，則

$$
X ^ {\prime \prime} (x) Y + X (x) Y ^ {\prime \prime} (y) = 0 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad \frac {X ^ {\prime \prime} (x)}{X (x)} = - \frac {Y ^ {\prime \prime} (y)}{Y (y)} = - \lambda
$$

因此 $X$ 、 $Y$ 满足二階常微分方程

$$
X ^ {\prime \prime} (x) + \lambda^ {2} X (x) = 0, \qquad Y ^ {\prime \prime} (y) - \lambda^ {2} Y (y) = 0
$$

因此可得

$$
X (x) = a _ {1} \cos \lambda x + b _ {1} \sin \lambda x, \qquad Y (y) = a _ {2} \cosh \lambda y + b _ {2} \sinh \lambda y
$$

$$
u (x, y) = X (x) Y (y) = \left(a _ {1} \cos \lambda x + b _ {1} \sin \lambda x\right) \left(a _ {2} \cosh \lambda y + b _ {2} \sinh \lambda y\right)
$$

另外由邊界值可得

$$
u (0, y) = 0 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad a _ {1} = 0
$$

$$
u (x, 0) = 0 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad a _ {2} = 0
$$

$$
u (1, y) = 0 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad \lambda = m \pi , \qquad m = 1, 2, 3, \dots
$$

因此其中一個解為

$$
u _ {m} (x, y) = \sin m \pi x \sinh m \pi y
$$

再由重疊原理可得一般解

$$
u (x, y) = \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {\infty} B _ {m} u _ {m} (x, y) = \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {\infty} B _ {m} \sin m \pi x \sinh m \pi y
$$

係數 $B_{m}$ 可由最後一個邊界值而得

$$
u (x, 1) = u _ {1} (x) = \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {\infty} B _ {m} \sinh m \pi \sin m \pi x
$$

利用 $\sin m\pi x$ 之正交性可得

$$
B _ {m} \sinh m \pi = 2 \int_ {0} ^ {1} u _ {1} (x) \sin m \pi x d x = \frac {2 u _ {1} (x) (1 - \cos m \pi)}{m \pi}
$$

$$
B _ {m} = \frac {2 u _ {1} (x) (1 - \cos m \pi)}{m \pi \sinh m \pi}
$$

故原調和函數的解爲

$$
u (x, y) = \frac {2 u _ {1} (x)}{\pi} \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {1 - \cos m \pi}{m \sinh m \pi} \sin m \pi \sinh m \pi y
$$

例題3.3.4.（環形區域）Laplace方程的邊界值問題

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = 0, \qquad (x, y) \in \Omega
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} u (x, y) = g, & (x, y) \in C _ {1} \\ u (x, y) = f, & (x, y) \in C _ {2} \end{array} \right.
$$

其中 $\Omega$ 為環狀區域，而 $C_{1}$ 、 $C_{2}$ 則為其邊界

$$
\Omega = \{(x, y) | a ^ {2} \leq x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} \leq b ^ {2} \}
$$

$$
C _ {1} = \{(x, y) | x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} = a ^ {2} \}, \qquad C _ {2} = \{(x, y) | x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} = b ^ {2} \}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/587142fa477092e61f4136e0944425188a9c550809fa631e9bece7d053712db8.jpg)



圖3.3.4


[解]: 我們先引進極座標(polar coordinate)

$$
x = r \cos \theta , \qquad y = r \sin \theta , \qquad r ^ {2} = x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}, \qquad \tan \theta = \frac {y}{x}
$$

則Laplace算子可化爲

$$
\Delta u = u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = u _ {r r} + \frac {1}{r} u _ {r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} u _ {\theta \theta} = \frac {1}{r} (r u _ {r}) _ {r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} u _ {\theta \theta}
$$

同時我們並要求

$$
u (r, \theta) = u (r, \theta + 2 \pi), \qquad a <   r <   b
$$

因此原問題化爲

(D.E.) 

$$
\frac {1}{r} (r u _ {r}) _ {r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} u _ {\theta \theta}, \qquad a <   r <   b, \qquad - \infty <   \theta <   \infty
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} u (a, \theta) = g (\theta), & u (b, \theta) = f (\theta), - \infty <   \theta <   \infty \\ g (\theta + 2 \pi) = g (\theta), & f (\theta + 2 \pi) = f (\theta) \end{array} \right.
$$

現在考慮分離變數 $u(r,\theta)=R(r)H(\theta)$ ，則H、R滿足

$$
H ^ {\prime \prime} + \lambda H = 0, \qquad - \infty <   \theta <   \infty
$$

$$
H (\theta + 2 \pi) = H (\theta), \qquad - \infty <   \theta <   \infty
$$

$$
r (r R ^ {\prime}) ^ {\prime} + R = 0, \quad a <   r <   b
$$

其固有值、固有函數為(經計算)

$$
\lambda_ {n} = n ^ {2}, \qquad n = 0, 1, 2, \dots
$$

$$
H _ {n} (\theta) = C _ {1, n} \cos n \theta + C _ {2, n} \sin n \theta , \qquad H _ {0} (\theta) = C _ {1, 0},
$$

$$
R _ {n} (r) = C _ {3, n} r ^ {n} + C _ {4, n} r ^ {- n}, \qquad R _ {0} (r) = C _ {3, 0} + C _ {4, 0} \log r,
$$

因此 $u_{n}(r,\theta) = R_{n}(r)H_{n}(\theta)$ 基本上是 $r^n\cos n\theta$ 、 $r^{-n}\cos n\theta$ 、 $r^n\sin n\theta$ 、 $r^{-n}\sin n\theta$ 之線性組合，至於 $u_{0}(r,\theta)$ 則為1、 $\log r$ 之線性組合，因此一般解可表爲

$$
u (r, \theta) = \frac {1}{2} (\alpha_ {0} + \beta_ {0} \log r) + \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \left[ (\alpha_ {n} r ^ {n} + \beta_ {n} r ^ {- n}) \cos n \theta + (\gamma_ {n} r ^ {n} + \delta_ {n} r ^ {- n}) \sin n \theta \right]
$$

係數 $\alpha_{0}$ 、 $\beta_{0}$ 、 $\alpha_{n}$ 、 $\beta_{n}$ 、 $\gamma_{n}$ 、 $\delta_{n}$ 可由邊界值 $u(a,\theta)=g(\theta)$ 、 $u(b,\theta)=f(\theta)$ 來決定。

例題3.3.5.（圓盤區域）Laplace方程的邊界值問題

(D.E.) 

$$
\Delta u = u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = 0, \qquad (x, y) \in B _ {R}
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u = f, \qquad (x, y) \in \partial B _ {R}
$$

其中 $B_{R} = \{(x,y)|x^{2} + y^{2}\leq R^{2}\}$ 表示半徑等於 $R$ 之圓盤。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/a2395660baf17a5c71a96075ab8e666f60448ee69e58064250a783137f4e4ebb.jpg)



圖3.3.5


[解]: 利用極座標原問題化為

(D.E.) 

$$
\frac {1}{r} (r u _ {r}) _ {r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} u _ {\theta \theta} = 0, \qquad 0 <   r <   R, \qquad 0 <   \theta <   2 \pi ,\tag{B.C.}
$$

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} u (R, \theta) = f (\theta), \qquad - \pi <   \theta <   \pi \\ u (r, \theta + 2 \pi) = u (r, \theta), \qquad 0 <   r <   R, \qquad - \pi <   \theta <   \pi \end{array} \right.
$$

利用分離係數法可得

$$
u (r, \theta) = \frac {1}{2} A _ {0} (r) + \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \Bigl (A _ {n} (r) \cos n \theta + B _ {n} (r) \sin n \theta \Bigr)
$$

其中

$$
\binom{A _ {n} (r)}{B _ {n} (r)} = \frac {1}{\pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} u (r, \theta) \binom{\cos n \theta}{\sin n \theta} d \theta
$$

我們現在決定係數 $A_{n}(r)$ 、 $B_{n}(r)$ 

$$
r \frac {d A _ {n}}{d r} = \frac {1}{\pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} r \frac {\partial u (r , \theta)}{\partial r} \cos n \theta
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {1}{r} \frac {d}{d r} (r \frac {d A _ {n}}{d r}) = \frac {1}{\pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} \frac {1}{r} \frac {\partial}{\partial r} (r \frac {\partial u (r , \theta)}{\partial r}) \cos n \theta d \theta} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{\pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} - \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} \frac {\partial^ {2} u (r , \theta)}{\partial \theta^ {2}} \cos n \theta d \theta \qquad (\text {利用方程式})} \\ & {\qquad = - \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} \frac {1}{\pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} \frac {\partial^ {2} u (r , \theta)}{\partial \theta^ {2}} \cos n \theta d \theta} \end{array}
$$

利用Green公式得

$$
\begin{array}{r} \frac {1}{r} \frac {d}{d r} (r \frac {d A _ {n}}{d r}) = - \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} \frac {1}{\pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} n \frac {d ^ {2}}{d \theta^ {2}} \cos n \theta d \theta \\ = \frac {n ^ {2}}{r ^ {2}} \frac {1}{\pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} n \cos n \theta = \frac {n ^ {2}}{r ^ {2}} A _ {n} \end{array}
$$

因此 $A_{n}$ 满足方程

$$
r ^ {2} \frac {d ^ {2} A _ {n}}{d r ^ {2}} + r \frac {d A _ {n}}{d r} - n ^ {2} A _ {n} = 0, \qquad 0 <   r <   R
$$

令 $r\to R$ ，則

$$
u (R, \theta) = f (\theta) \quad \Longrightarrow \quad A _ {n} (R) = \alpha_ {n}
$$

$$
\binom{\alpha_ {n}}{\beta_ {n}} = \frac {1}{\pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} f (\theta) \binom{\cos n \theta}{\sin n \theta} d \theta
$$

$A_{n}$ 滿足 Euler 方程，其解可表爲

$$
A _ {n} (r) = C _ {1, n} r ^ {n} + C _ {2, n} r ^ {- n}, \qquad n = 1, 2, \ldots
$$

$$
A _ {0} (r) = C _ {1, 0} + C _ {2, 0} \log r,
$$

但

$$
\log r \to - \infty , \qquad r ^ {- n} \to + \infty , \qquad \mathrm{當} r \to 0 _ {+}
$$

因此要求

$$
C _ {2, n} = 0, \qquad n = 0, 1, 2, \dots
$$

令 $r\to 0^{+}$ ，得

$$
\lim _ {r \to 0 ^ {+}} A _ {n} (r) = \frac {u _ {0}}{\pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} \cos n \theta d \theta = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 2 u _ {0}, & n = 0 \\ 0, & n = 1, 2, \dots \end{array} \right.
$$

故

$$
A _ {n} (r) = C _ {n} r ^ {n}, \qquad n = 0, 1, 2, \dots
$$

由 $A_{n}(b) = \alpha_{n}$ ，可得 $A_{n}(r) = \alpha_{n}\frac{r^{n}}{R^{n}}$ ，同理可得 $B_{n}(r) = \beta_{n}\frac{r^{n}}{R^{n}}$ ，故原微分方程之解爲

$$
u (r, \theta) = \frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {0} + \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \left(\frac {r}{R}\right) ^ {n} (\alpha_ {n} \cos n \theta + \beta_ {n} \sin n \theta)
$$

例題3.3.6.（長方形區域之固有值問題） $\Omega = [0,l]\times [0,m]$ 

$$
- \Delta u = - u _ {x x} - u _ {y y} = \lambda u, \qquad u (x, y) \Big | _ {\partial \Omega} = 0
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/3014aafc22f953abb22508ef030f6e4c8030bf4a1fe4f9c6afbf20aab451422a.jpg)



圖 3.3.6


[解]: 由分離變數法，設 $u(x,y)=X(x)Y(y)$ 則

$$
\begin{array}{l l} - X ^ {\prime \prime} = \mu X, & X (0) = X (l) = 0, \\ - Y ^ {\prime \prime} = \nu Y, & Y (0) = Y (m) = 0, \end{array}
$$

分別解 $X$ 與 $Y$ 得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mu_ {k} = \left(\frac {k \pi}{l}\right) ^ {2}, \qquad X _ {k} (x) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{l}} \sin \frac {k \pi x}{l}, \qquad k = 1, 2, 3, \dots \\ \nu_ {j} = \left(\frac {j \pi}{m}\right) ^ {2}, \qquad Y _ {j} (y) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{m}} \sin \frac {j \pi y}{m}, \qquad j = 1, 2, 3, \dots \end{array}
$$

因此原Laplace方程之固有值與固有函數爲

$$
\lambda_ {k j} = \mu_ {k} + \nu_ {j} = \pi^ {2} \left(\frac {k ^ {2}}{l ^ {2}} + \frac {j ^ {2}}{m ^ {2}}\right),
$$

$$
u _ {k j} (x, y) = X _ {k} (x) Y _ {j} (y) = \frac {2}{\sqrt {l m}} \sin {\frac {k \pi x}{l}} \sin {\frac {j \pi y}{m}}
$$

我們容許固有值是重根 $\lambda_{kj} = \lambda_{k_0j_0}$ 

$$
\frac {k ^ {2}}{l ^ {2}} + \frac {j ^ {2}}{m ^ {2}} = \frac {k _ {0} ^ {2}}{l ^ {2}} + \frac {j _ {0} ^ {2}}{m ^ {2}}
$$

## 習題3.3

1. 試解 Laplace 方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   \pi , \qquad 0 <   y <   1
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} u _ {x} (0, y) = u _ {x} (\pi , y) = 0, & 0 \leq y \leq 1 \\ u (x, 0) = 4 \cos 6 x + \cos 7 x, & u (x, 1) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq \pi \end{array} \right.
$$

[解答]: $u(x,y) = \frac{4\cos 6x\sinh(6(y - 1))}{\sinh(-6)} +\frac{\cos 7x\sinh(7(y - 1))}{\sinh(-7)}$ 

2. 試解 Laplace 方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = 0, \qquad 0 <   x <   \pi , \qquad 0 <   y <   \pi
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} u (0, y) = u (\pi , y) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq y \leq \pi \\ u (x, 0) = f (x), \qquad u (x, \pi) = 0, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq \pi \end{array} \right.
$$

[解答]: $u(x,y) = \sum_{n=1}^{\infty} a_n \sin nx \sinh (ny - n\pi)$ 

$$
a _ {n} = \frac {2}{\pi \sinh (- n \pi)} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} f (x) \sin n x d x
$$

3. 試解 Laplace 方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = 0, \quad 0 <   x <   \pi , \quad 0 <   y <   1
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} u _ {x} (0, y) = u _ {x} (\pi , y) = 0, & 0 \leq y \leq 1 \\ u (x, 0) = \cos x - \cos 3 x, & u (x, 1) = \cos 2 x, \qquad 0 \leq x \leq \pi \end{array} \right.
$$

[解答]: $u(x,y) = \frac{\cos x\sinh(y - 1)}{\sinh(-1)} -\frac{\cos 3x\sinh(3y - 3)}{\sinh(-3)} +\frac{\cos 2x\sinh 2y}{\sinh(2)}$ 

4. 試解 Laplace 方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {r r} + \frac {1}{r} u _ {r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} u _ {\theta \theta} = 0, \qquad 0 \leq r <   2, \qquad - \pi \leq \theta \leq \pi
$$

(B.C.) $u(2,\theta) = |\theta |,\quad -\pi \leq \theta \leq \pi$ 

[解答]: $u(r, \theta) = \frac{\pi}{2} - \sum_{n=1}^{\infty} \frac{r^{2n+1}}{(2n + 1)^2 \pi 2^{2n-1}} \cos(2n + 1)\theta$ 

5. 試解 Laplace 方程式

$$
u _ {r r} + \frac {1}{r} u _ {r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} u _ {\theta \theta} = 0, \qquad 0 \leq r <   a, \qquad - \pi \leq \theta \leq \pi\tag{D.E.}
$$

$$
(\mathrm{B.C.}) \qquad u _ {r} (a, \theta) = f (\theta), \qquad - \pi \leq \theta \leq \pi
$$

[解答]: $u(r, \theta) = \frac{a_0}{2} + \sum_{n=1}^{\infty} \left(\frac{r}{a}\right)^n (a_n \cos n\theta + b_n \sin n\theta)$ 

$$
a _ {n} = \frac {a}{n \pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} f (\theta) \cos n \theta d \theta , b _ {n} = \frac {a}{n \pi} \int_ {- \pi} ^ {\pi} f (\theta) \sin n \theta d \theta
$$

6. 試解 Laplace 方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {r r} + \frac {1}{r} u _ {r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} u _ {\theta \theta} = 0, \qquad 1 \leq r <   2, \qquad - \pi \leq \theta \leq \pi
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (1, \theta) = \sin 4 \theta - \cos \theta , \quad u (2, \theta) = \sin \theta , \quad - \pi \leq \theta \leq \pi
$$

[解答]: $u(r, \theta) = \left(\frac{1}{3} r - \frac{4}{3} r^{-1}\right) \cos \theta + \left(\frac{2}{3} r - \frac{2}{3} r^{-1}\right) \sin \theta$ 

$$
+ \left(- \frac {1}{2 5 5} r ^ {4} + \frac {2 5 6}{2 5 5} r ^ {- 4}\right) \sin 4 \theta
$$

## 7. 試解 Laplace 方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {r r} + \frac {1}{r} u _ {r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} u _ {\theta \theta} = 0, \qquad a \leq r <   b, \qquad - \infty \leq \theta \leq \infty
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (a, \theta) = u (b, \theta) = 0, \quad - \infty \leq \theta \leq \infty
$$

[解答]: $u(r, \theta) = \sin \left(n \pi \frac{\log r - \log a}{\log b - \log a}\right) \exp \left(\frac{n \pi \theta}{\log b - \log a}\right), \quad n = 1, 2, \cdots$ 

8. 試解 Poisson 方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = - x y, \quad 0 \leq r <   R
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (R, \theta) = 0, \quad - \pi <   \theta <   \pi
$$

[解答]: $u(r,\theta) = -\frac{1}{24} r^3 \sin 2\theta + \frac{R^4}{48\pi} \int_{-\pi}^{\pi} \sin 2t \frac{R^2 - r^2}{R^2 - 2Rr \cos(t - \theta) + R^2} dt$ 

9. 試解 Poisson 方程式

(D.E.) 

$$
u _ {x x} + u _ {y y} = 1 2 (x ^ {2} - y ^ {2}), \qquad a \leq r \leq b, \qquad - \pi <   \theta <   \pi
$$

(B.C.) 

$$
u (a, \theta) = 0, \quad u _ {r} (b, \theta) = 0, \quad - \pi <   \theta <   \pi
$$

[解答]： $u(r,\theta) = \left[(a^4 +b^4)r^4 -(a^6 +2b^6)r^2 -(a^2 -2b^2)\frac{a^4b^4}{r^2}\right]\frac{\cos 2\theta}{a^4 + b^4}$ 

## § 3.4 Schrödinger 方程式

量子力學的代表Schrödinger方程式不是由實驗直接推導，所以無法像牛頓力學或電磁學的Maxwell方程經由實驗推論而得。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/5d1a2795520380c618f692629797d0a8009b47f1b405edaa1e025062c2e2f3b0.jpg)



圖 3.4.1 de Broglie


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/dced5c98c7d57b710149ea01989e6c797a25e69ce68944b619120e30bcbc51d4.jpg)



圖 3.4.2 Schrödinger



量子力學最好的起點是de Broglie (1892-1987)的物質波公式


$$
\lambda = \frac {\hbar}{m v}, \qquad E = \hbar \nu\tag{3.4.1}
$$

其中 $\lambda$ 是物質波的波長、 $\nu$ 則是物質波之頻率、 $\hbar$ 是普朗克常數、m 是質量、v 則是速度。為何 $\lambda$ 是波長、 $\nu$ 是頻率呢？首先必須明白普朗克常數 $\hbar$ ，開宗明義而言

$$
\hbar \approx S: \mathrm{作用量} (\mathrm{action})\tag{3.4.2}
$$

按古典力學作用量是Lagrangian對時間t的積分，而Lagrangian是動能與位能的差， $L = T - V$ 、 $T(\text{動能})$ 、 $V(\text{位能})$ ，所以 $\hbar$ 或作用量的量綱為

$$
[ \hbar ] = [ \int \mathcal {L} d t ] = [ \mathcal {L} ] [ t ] = \mathrm{ML} ^ {2} \mathrm{T} ^ {- 2} \mathrm{T} = \mathrm{ML} ^ {2} \mathrm{T} ^ {- 1}\tag{3.4.3}
$$

一旦知道 $\hbar$ 之量綱則 $\lambda$ 與 $\nu$ 之量綱分別等於

$$
[ \lambda ] = \frac {[ \hbar ]}{[ m v ]} = \frac {\mathrm{ML} ^ {2} \mathrm{T} ^ {- 1}}{\mathrm{MLT} ^ {- 1}} = \mathrm{L}\tag{3.4.4}
$$

$$
[ \nu ] = \frac {[ E ]}{[ \hbar ]} = \frac {\mathrm{ML} ^ {2} \mathrm{T} ^ {- 2}}{\mathrm{ML} ^ {2} \mathrm{T} ^ {- 1}} = \mathrm{T} ^ {- 1}\tag{3.4.5}
$$

所以由量綱分析的角度來看 $\lambda$ 的確是波長，而 $\nu$ 則是頻率，de Broglie 物質波公式(3.4.1)，另外有趣的一件事是： $\lambda$ 、 $\nu$ 是波的行爲，而能量 $E$ 與動量 $p = mv$ 則是粒子之行爲，但在微觀世界，即 $\hbar$ 出現時，兩者的行爲都是有可能的（所謂的波—粒二象性），而且波—粒子是藉由普朗克常數 $\hbar$ 來連繫，因此我們可以說普朗克常數 $\hbar$ 是波動性與粒子性之間的橋樑。

我們可以藉由de Broglie公式(3.4.1)將所有粒子行爲的量表示爲波的量，例如 $T(\text{動能})$ 、 $V(\text{位能})$ 、 $E = T + V = \frac{1}{2}mv^{2} + V(\text{總能})$ ，則

$$
E = \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} + V = \hbar \nu , \qquad \hbar \nu = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m \lambda^ {2}} + V\tag{3.4.6}
$$

一般的波函数可表示爲

$$
\Phi (x, t) = A \cos 2 \pi \left(\frac {x}{\lambda} - \nu t - \delta\right)\tag{3.4.7}
$$

這裡 $\lambda$ 、 $\nu$ 是波質波之波長與頻率， $\delta$ 是無量綱 (dimensionless) 代表相位移， $A$ 代表振幅 (amplitude)，由量綱分析的角度而言，這個假設是合理的

$$
[ \lambda ] = \mathrm{L}, \qquad [ \nu ] = \mathrm{T} ^ {- 1}, \qquad [ \delta ] = 1
$$

直接微分得

$$
\frac {\partial \Phi}{\partial t} = 2 \pi \nu A \sin 2 \pi \left(\frac {x}{\lambda} - \nu t - \delta\right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial t ^ {2}} = - 4 \pi^ {2} \nu^ {2} A \cos 2 \pi \left(\frac {x}{\lambda} - \nu t - \delta\right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \Phi}{\partial x} = - \frac {2 \pi}{\lambda} A \sin 2 \pi \left(\frac {x}{\lambda} - \nu t - \delta\right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial x ^ {2}} = - \frac {4 \pi^ {2}}{\lambda^ {2}} A \cos 2 \pi \left(\frac {x}{\lambda} - \nu t - \delta\right)
$$

所以

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial t ^ {2}} = - 4 \pi^ {2} \nu^ {2} \Phi , \quad \frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial x ^ {2}} = - \frac {4 \pi^ {2}}{\lambda^ {2}} \Phi
$$

將de Broglie公式(3.4.1)代入得

$$
\hbar \nu \Phi = - \frac {\hbar}{4 \pi^ {2} \nu} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial t ^ {2}}, \quad \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m \lambda^ {2}} \Phi = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{8 \pi^ {2} m} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial x ^ {2}}\tag{3.4.8}
$$

但是總能量 $E = \hbar\nu = \frac{\hbar^2}{2m\lambda^2} + V$ ，所以

$$
\begin{array}{r} E \Phi = \hbar \nu \Phi = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m \lambda^ {2}} \Phi + V \Phi \\ - \frac {\hbar}{4 \pi^ {2} \nu} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial t ^ {2}} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{8 \pi^ {2} m} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial x ^ {2}} + V \Phi \end{array}\tag{3.4.9}
$$

另外由於

$$
\frac {\hbar}{4 \pi^ {2} \nu} \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m}{\hbar^ {2}} = \frac {2 m}{\nu \hbar} = \frac {2}{\lambda^ {2} \nu^ {2}}, \qquad v = \lambda \nu
$$

所以得Schrödinger方程

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial x ^ {2}} - \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m}{\hbar^ {2}} V \Phi = \frac {2}{\lambda^ {2} \nu^ {2}} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial t ^ {2}}\tag{3.4.10}
$$

如果是 $n$ 維空間，則將 $\frac{\partial^2}{\partial x^2}$ 換為 $\Delta = \frac{\partial^2}{\partial x_1^2} +\frac{\partial^2}{\partial x_2^2} +\dots +\frac{\partial^2}{\partial x_n^2}$ 

$$
\Delta \Phi - \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m}{\hbar^ {2}} V \Phi = \frac {2}{\lambda^ {2} \nu^ {2}} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Phi}{\partial t ^ {2}}\tag{3.4.11}
$$

由Bernoulli的分離變數法可以假設 $(n = 3)$ 

$$
\Phi = A \cos 2 \pi \nu (t - \delta) \psi (x, y, z)\tag{3.4.12}
$$

代入(3.4.11)則ψ滿足

$$
\Delta \psi + 8 \pi^ {2} \left(\frac {1}{\lambda^ {2}} - \frac {m}{\hbar^ {2}} V\right) \psi = 0\tag{3.4.13}
$$

由於

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \lambda m v = \hbar \\ \lambda^ {2} m E = \hbar^ {2} \end{array} \right. \Rightarrow \left\{ \begin{array}{l} \lambda^ {2} m \nu = \hbar \\ \frac {1}{\lambda^ {2}} = \frac {m E}{\hbar^ {2}} \end{array} \right.
$$

所以(3.4.13)可以改寫爲

$$
\Delta \psi + \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m}{\hbar^ {2}} (E - V) \psi = 0\tag{3.4.14}
$$

這個方程式是量綱平衡

$$
\Delta \approx \frac {1}{\mathrm{L} ^ {2}}, \qquad \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m}{\hbar^ {2}} \approx \frac {\mathrm{M}}{(\mathrm{ML} ^ {2} \mathrm{T} ^ {- 1}) ^ {2}} \mathrm{ML} ^ {2} \mathrm{T} ^ {- 2} = \frac {1}{\mathrm{L} ^ {2}}
$$

通常我們將(3.4.14)改寫為 $(x_{i}\to 2\pi x_{i})$ 

$$
\bigg (- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \Delta + V \bigg) \psi = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \Delta \psi + V \psi = E \psi\tag{3.4.15}
$$

從Sturn-Liouville問題的角度而言，波函數 $\psi$ 的意義就是固有函數(eigenfunction)，而能量 $E$ 則是固有值(eigenvalue)。令

$$
\Psi (x _ {1}, \dots , x _ {n}, t) = \psi (x _ {1}, \dots , x _ {n}) e ^ {- i E t / \hbar}, \qquad [ E t ] = [ \hbar ]\tag{3.4.16}
$$

則 $\Psi(\text{稱為stationary state})$ 滿足 Schrödinger 方程 (帶時間)

$$
i \hbar \partial_ {t} \Psi = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \Delta \Psi + V \Psi\tag{3.4.17}
$$

Schrödinger 方程

$$
\frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \psi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + [ E - V (x) ] \psi (x) = 0\tag{3.4.18}
$$

實際上就是Sturm-Liouville方程

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + [ \lambda - q (x) ] \psi (x) = 0, \\ \lambda = \frac {2 m E}{\hbar^ {2}}, \qquad q (x) = \frac {2 m V (x)}{\hbar^ {2}} \end{array}\tag{3.4.19}
$$

在量子力學中我們討論的無窮區間 $(-∞,∞)$ ，因此(3.4.19)稱為奇異Sturm-Liouville問題。

例題3.4.1.（自由粒子：free particle）

$$
\psi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + \lambda \psi (x) = 0, \qquad - \infty <   x <   \infty
$$

[解]：(1) $\lambda > 0$ ，Schrödinger方程兩組有界的解（固有函數）

$$
\sin \sqrt {\lambda} x, \qquad \cos \sqrt {\lambda} x
$$

(2) 若 $\lambda = 0$ ，則有 $u = 1$ 的有界解

(3) $\lambda < 0$ ，則爲

$$
\sinh \sqrt {\lambda} x, \qquad \cosh \sqrt {\lambda} x
$$

但這兩組都不是有界。因此對自由粒子的Schrödinger方程而言，固有值是 $\lambda \geq 0$ ，而且是連續稱為連續(頻)值譜，頻譜是連續的主要原因是所在的空間是無窮區間 $(- \infty, \infty)$ 。

例題3.4.2.（無限方井位：infinite square-well potential）

$$
\psi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + [ \lambda - V (x) ] \psi (x) = 0, \qquad - \infty <   x <   \infty
$$

$$
V (x) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 0, & 0 \leq x \leq a \\ \infty , & \text {其它} \end{array} \right.
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/78fee41ea0d2b7083219c8e5bfc1116520ba68ed9b72a1b79e3ec55e7e0935e1.jpg)



圖 3.4.3


[解]: 顯然必須要求 $\lambda > 0 (E > 0)$ ，方程的解是

$$
\psi (x) = A \cos {\sqrt {\lambda}} x + B \sin {\sqrt {\lambda}} x
$$

而且滿足邊界條件

$$
\psi (0) = \psi (a) = 0
$$

所以固有值與固有函數

$$
\lambda_ {n} = \left(\frac {n \pi}{a}\right) ^ {2}, \qquad E _ {n} = \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}}, \qquad n = 1, 2, 3, \dots
$$

$$
\psi_ {n} (x) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \sin \left(\frac {n \pi x}{a}\right), \qquad n = 1, 2, \dots ,
$$

此時對應的stationary state爲

$$
\Psi_ {n} (x, t) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \sin \left(\frac {n \pi x}{a}\right) e ^ {- i (n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar / 2 m a ^ {2}) t}, \qquad n = 1, 2, \dots ,
$$

所以 Schrödinger 方程(帶時間)

$$
i \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \Psi (x, t) = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x ^ {2}} \Psi (x, t) + V \Psi (x, t)
$$

的解爲

$$
\Psi (x, t) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} c _ {n} \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \sin \left(\frac {n \pi x}{a}\right) e ^ {- i (n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar / 2 m a ^ {2}) t}
$$

其中係數 $c_{n}$ 由初始值決定

$$
\Psi (x, 0) = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} c _ {n} \psi_ {n} (x), \qquad c _ {n} = \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \sin \left(\frac {n \pi x}{a}\right) \Psi (x, 0) d x
$$

例题3.4.3.（方井位：square-well potential）

$$
\psi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + [ \lambda - V (x) ] \psi (x) = 0, \qquad - \infty <   x <   \infty
$$

$$
V (x) = \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} - c ^ {2}, & | x | <   a \\ 0, & | x | > a \end{array} \right.
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/5c24c1f2-d8d9-4a3d-939d-7dd1e87cc28e/9397508af852d983a9681e2b62ec3347e69d39fbeb9bfdda66a95bd8b193ec8b.jpg)



圖 3.4.4


[解]: 由對稱性容易判斷若 $\psi(x)$ 是固有函數，則 $\psi(-x)$ 也是固有函數。同理， $u$ 之偶函數與奇函數部份都會是固有函數。

(1) $\lambda > 0$ : 方程的解是

$$
A \cos {\sqrt {\lambda}} x + B \sin {\sqrt {\lambda}} x
$$

所以當 $|x| > a$ 值譜是連續的。

(2) 若 $\lambda < -c^2$ ，方程的解是 $\cosh \sqrt{-\lambda - c^2} x$ 與 $\sinh \sqrt{-\lambda - c^2} x$ 之組合，但這兩個解都不是有界，所以固有值不存在。

(3) $-c^{2}<\lambda<0$ ，此時可以證明固有值是離散的。

諧振子方程(harmonic oscillator)是量子力學的Schrodinger方程中最出名的一個。

例題3.4.4.（諧振子：harmonic oscillator）

$$
\psi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + (\lambda - x ^ {2}) \psi (x) = 0, \qquad - \infty <   x <   \infty
$$

[解]: 諧振子方程實際上就是Hermite方程

$$
\psi^ {\prime \prime} (x) + [ (\lambda - 1) - (x ^ {2} - 1) ] \psi (x) = 0, \qquad - \infty <   x <   \infty
$$

所以固有值與固有函數為

$$
\lambda_ {n} = 2 n + 1, \qquad \psi_ {n} (x) = e ^ {- \frac {x ^ {2}}{2}} H _ {n} (x), \qquad n = 0, 1, 2, \dots
$$

而且這些固有函數是平方可積， $\psi_n \in L^2(-\infty, \infty)$ 。

例題3.4.6.(氫原子模型)

$$
\Delta \psi + \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m}{\hbar^ {2}} \bigg (\hbar \nu + \frac {e ^ {2}}{r} \bigg) \psi = 0\tag{3.4.20}
$$

這裡 e 是原子核之電荷，-e 是電子之電荷，r 則是原子核與電子之距離， $V = -\frac{e^{2}}{r}$ 代表電位（庫侖定律）。

[解]：因爲電位 $V$ 只與半徑有關，所以直接的聯想就是球座標

$$
x = r \sin \varphi \cos \theta , \qquad y = r \sin \varphi \sin \theta , \qquad z = r \cos \varphi
$$

所以 Schrödinger 方程式(3.4.20)可以改寫爲

$$
\begin{array}{r} \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} \frac {\partial}{\partial r} \bigg (r ^ {2} \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial r} \bigg) + \frac {1}{r ^ {2} \sin \varphi} \frac {\partial}{\partial \varphi} \bigg (\sin \varphi \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial \varphi} \bigg) \\ + \frac {1}{r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \varphi} \frac {\partial^ {2} \psi}{\partial \theta^ {2}} + \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m}{\hbar^ {2}} \bigg (\hbar \nu + \frac {e ^ {2}}{r} \bigg) \psi = 0 \end{array}\tag{3.4.21}
$$

由Bernoulli分離變數法可以假設

$$
\psi (r, \varphi , \theta) = R (r) \Phi (\varphi) \Theta (\theta)
$$

代入(3.4.21)得

$$
\begin{array}{r} \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} (r ^ {2} R ^ {\prime \prime} \Theta \Phi + 2 r R ^ {\prime} \Theta \Phi) + \frac {\cot \varphi}{r ^ {2}} R \Theta \Phi^ {\prime} + \frac {R \Theta \Phi^ {\prime \prime}}{r ^ {2}} \\ + \frac {1}{r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \varphi} R \Theta^ {\prime \prime} \Phi + \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m}{\hbar^ {2}} \bigg (\hbar \nu + \frac {e ^ {2}}{r} \bigg) R \Theta \Phi = 0 \end{array}
$$

除以 $R\Theta \Phi$ 之後乘 $r^2$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {r ^ {2} R ^ {\prime \prime}}{R} + \frac {2 r R ^ {\prime}}{R} + r ^ {2} \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m}{\hbar^ {2}} \bigg (\hbar \nu + \frac {e ^ {2}}{r} \bigg)} \\ & {= - \cot \varphi \frac {\Phi^ {\prime}}{\Phi} - \frac {\Phi^ {\prime \prime}}{\Phi} - \frac {1}{\sin^ {2} \varphi} \frac {\Theta^ {\prime \prime}}{\Theta} \equiv n (n + 1)} \end{array}\tag{3.4.22}
$$

假設 $\Theta$ 滿足二階微分方程

$$
\Theta^ {\prime \prime} + \mu^ {2} \Theta = 0, \qquad \mu \in \mathbb {R}\tag{3.4.23}
$$

則 $\Phi$ 滿足微分方程

$$
\sin^ {2} \varphi \Phi^ {\prime \prime} + \sin \varphi \cos \varphi \Phi^ {\prime} + n (n + 1) \sin^ {2} \varphi \Phi - \mu^ {2} \Phi = 0\tag{3.4.24}
$$

再令 $\xi = \cos \varphi$ 、 $\Phi (\varphi) = P(\xi)$ ，則 $P$ 正是Legendre方程的解

$$
\frac {d}{d \xi} \bigg [ (1 - \xi^ {2}) \frac {d P}{d \xi} \bigg ] + \bigg [ n (n + 1) - \frac {\nu^ {2}}{1 - \xi^ {2}} \bigg ] P = 0\tag{3.4.25}
$$

<!-- === Source: MinerU_markdown_傅里叶分析讲义_201_2122709143092572173.md (Page 201) === -->

所以 $\Phi$ 與 $\Theta$ 表爲

$$
\Phi (\varphi) = P _ {n} ^ {\mu} (\cos \varphi), \qquad \Theta = A \cos \mu \theta + B \sin \mu \theta ,
$$

$$
\Theta (\theta + 2 \pi) = \Theta (\theta), \quad \mu = 0, \pm 1, \pm 2, \dots
$$

現在討論R部份

$$
R ^ {\prime \prime} + \frac {2 R ^ {\prime}}{r} + \left(\frac {8 \pi^ {2} m}{\hbar^ {2}} \hbar \nu + \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m e ^ {2}}{\hbar^ {2} r} - \frac {n (n + 1)}{r ^ {2}}\right) R = 0\tag{3.4.26}
$$

爲了簡便令 $\frac{8\pi^2m}{\hbar^2}\hbar \nu$ 、 $\frac{8\pi^2me^2}{\hbar^2} = b$ ，則

$$
\frac {R ^ {\prime \prime} + 2 R ^ {\prime}}{r} + \left(a + \frac {b}{r} - \frac {n (n + 1)}{r ^ {2}}\right) R = 0\tag{3.4.27}
$$

再令 $k = \frac{b}{2\sqrt{-a}}$ 、 $z = 2\sqrt{-ar}$ ，所以

$$
\frac {d ^ {2} R}{d z ^ {2}} + \frac {2}{z} \frac {d R}{d z} + \left(- \frac {1}{4} + \frac {k}{z} - \frac {n (n + 1)}{z ^ {2}}\right) R = 0\tag{3.4.28}
$$

這就是Laguerre方程

$$
\frac {d}{d z} (z ^ {2} R ^ {\prime}) - \frac {z ^ {2} - 4 k z + 4 n (n + 1)}{4} R = 0\tag{3.4.29}
$$

## [註解]:

(1) 1924年L. deBroglie首創物質波之理論而後Schrödinger(1926)研究此問題，他的主要目的如下：

(a) 求一個 deBroglie 波的理論，或此波所遵守的方程式。

(b) 創一新的力學在極限情形之下趨近於古典力學，有如古典波動光學在波長λ很短之極限時趨近幾何光學。

Schrödinger並不問物質波是怎樣的一種波，卻著手寫出物質波傳播所依據的數學方程式—Schrödinger方程來描述物質波的傳播。他的出發點是古典力學的Hamilton-Jacobi方程式，

$$
\frac {\partial S}{\partial t} + H \left(q, \frac {\partial S}{\partial q}\right) = 0\tag{3.4.30}
$$

$S$ 為作用量（action）

$$
S = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t} L d t = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t} (T - V) d t\tag{3.4.31}
$$

仿古典力學之方法引進Hamilton特徵函數

$$
S = S _ {0} - E t \quad E = \text {能量} = \text {常數}\tag{3.4.32}
$$

則(3.4.30)式可改寫爲

$$
| \nabla S _ {0} | ^ {2} = 2 m (E - V) \frac {\partial S}{\partial t} = - E\tag{3.4.33}
$$

另外由全微分知

$$
d S = | \nabla S | d r + \frac {\partial S}{\partial t} d t = (\nabla S u - E) d t\tag{3.4.34}
$$

因此相速度（phase velocity）爲

$$
u = \frac {E}{| \nabla S _ {0} |} = \frac {E}{\sqrt {2 m (E - V)}}\tag{3.4.35}
$$

但粒子的速度爲

$$
v = \frac {1}{m} \sqrt {2 m (E - V)}\tag{3.4.36}
$$

v 顯然與 u 不同，但 Schrödinger 相信這個差異是極重要的，由於古典波動力學在波長 $\lambda \rightarrow 0$ 之極限時趨近於幾何光學（相對應於波動方程式趨近 Hamilton-Jacobi 方程式，故 Schrödinger 認為若要得一相當於波動力學之解，應從 Hamilton 原理著手。

## 習題3.4

1. 證明若 $E \leq 0$ 則Schrödinger方程

$$
\psi^ {\prime \prime} (x) = - \frac {2 m E}{\hbar^ {2}} \psi (x), \qquad \psi (0) = \psi (a) = 0
$$

$$
V (x) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 0, & - a / 2 <   x <   a / 2 \\ \infty , & \text {其它} \end{array} \right.
$$

沒有可接受的解。

2. 試解 Schrödinger 方程

$$
\psi^ {\prime \prime} (x) = \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} [ V (x) - E ] \psi (x), \qquad - \infty <   x <   \infty
$$

$$
V (x) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 0, & - a / 2 <   x <   a / 2 \\ \infty , & \text {其它} \end{array} \right.
$$

[解答]: $k = \frac{\sqrt{2mE}}{\hbar} = \frac{n\pi}{a}, n = 1, 2, 3, \cdots$ 

$$
\psi_ {n} (x) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {{\sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \sin \frac {n \pi x}{a},}} & {{m: \mathrm{偶數}}} \\ {{\sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \cos \frac {n \pi x}{a},}} & {{m: \mathrm{奇數}}} \end{array} \right.
$$

3. 試求 Schrödinger 方程

$$
\psi^ {\prime \prime} (x) = \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} [ V (x) - E ] \psi (x), \qquad - \infty <   x <   \infty
$$

$$
V (x) = \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} \frac {1}{2} m w ^ {2} x ^ {2}, & x > 0 \\ \infty , & x <   0 \end{array} \right.
$$

可能的能階 $E?$ 

[解答]: $E_{n}=(n+\frac{1}{2})\hbar w,n=1,3,5,\cdots$ 

## 4. 證明波函數

$$
\Psi (x, t) = \left(\frac {m w}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left[ - \frac {m w}{2 \hbar} \Bigl (x ^ {2} + \frac {a ^ {2}}{2} (1 + e ^ {- 2 i w t}) + \frac {i \hbar t}{m} - 2 a x e ^ {- i w t} \Bigr) \right]
$$

滿足諧振子Schrödinger方程

$$
i \hbar \frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial t} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Psi}{\partial x ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{2} m w ^ {2} x ^ {2} \Psi
$$

## 5. 證明波函數

$$
\Psi (x, t) = \frac {\sqrt {m \alpha}}{\hbar} e ^ {- m \alpha | x - v t | / \hbar^ {2}} e ^ {- i [ (E + (1 / 2) m v ^ {2}) t - m v x ] / \hbar}
$$

滿足 Schrödinger 方程

$$
i \hbar \frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial t} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Psi}{\partial x ^ {2}} - \alpha \delta (x - v t) \Psi
$$

## § 3.5 等周長不等式

等周問題(isoperimetric problem)是促進數學中變分法(Calculus of Variation)早期發展的一個重要問題。在17～18世紀曾經被瑞士Bernoulli家族的數學家們作為一個課題研究過。其中最著名的是極速線問題(Brachistochrone problem)，這是由Johann Bernoulli(1667-1748)所提的：當時，這個問題吸引了全歐洲居領導地位的數學家：牛頓、萊布尼茲、Jacob Bernoulli、L'Hospital、…。儘管Bernoulli兄弟，牛頓及同時代的其他人利用特殊的技巧解決了最速下降曲線問題，但到了十八、十九世紀Euler(1707-1783)，Lagrange(1736-1813)，Weierstrass(1815-1897)，Ostrogradskii(1801-1862)等人之努力才發展成完整的理論。

Eular與Lagrange成功地將變分學的問題化為微分方程(Euler-Lagrange方程)的問題，Lagrange並成功地應用到牛頓力學。最後愛爾蘭物理學家Sir William Rowan Hamilton(1805-1865)引進Hamiltonian函數將Euler-Lagrange方程簡化為Hamilton系統(Hamiltonian system)，這方法不僅解決牛頓力學並成功地應用到量子力學。變分法跟物理學上的最小作用量原理有關，作用量實際上就是動能減去位(勢)能。作用量原理講的是，粒子從一點運動到另一點，必然自覺自願選擇走作用量最小的路徑。與牛頓的三個力學定律相比，作用量原理，只有一條原則，顯得更加緊湊，最小作用量原理堪稱物理學中最美妙的一個原理。若 $L$ 為曲線之長度， $A$ 為其所圍之面積，則 $L$ 與 $A$ 之關係為

$$
A \leq \frac {L ^ {2}}{4 \pi}
$$

這個不等式稱為等周長不等式(isoperimetric inequality):

具有固定長度的所有閉的簡單平面曲線中圓所圍的面積最大。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/601c94dd-2c2c-4440-beb4-01d26aedce94/7c0a264f9a65cfebf6894b85ff59c67c0a4a71f3a30f2043626afcfa42b159b4.jpg)



圖3.5.1


定理3.5.1.（平面曲線的等周長不等式）平面上具有固定長度的簡單曲線中，圓所圍之面積為最大。換言之，若 $L$ 是簡單封閉曲線 $C$ 的長度， $A$ 是曲線 $C$ 所圍的面積，則

$$
L ^ {2} - 4 \pi A \geq 0\tag{3.5.1}
$$

且等號成立時，封閉曲線C必須是一個圓。

[證明]：如圖所示，設 $g, g'$ 為兩條平行直線與C相切於P, Q兩點，而 $s = 0, s_0$ 則分別是P, Q之參數。另外我們作一半徑為r的圓 $\bar{C}$ 與直線 $g, g'$ 分別相切於 $\bar{P}, \bar{Q}$ 兩點，假設其圓心為原點。命C之參數式為

$$
\varphi (s) = (x (s), y (s)), \qquad (x (0), y (0)) = (x (L), y (L))
$$

而 $\bar{C}$ 之參數式爲

$$
\bar {\varphi} (s) = (\bar {x} (s), \bar {y} (s))
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} \bar {x} (s) = x (s) \\ \bar {y} (s) = \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} - \sqrt {r ^ {2} - x ^ {2} (s)}, & 0 \leq s \leq s _ {0} \\ \sqrt {r ^ {2} - x ^ {2} (s)}, & s _ {0} \leq s \leq L \end{array} \right. \end{array}\tag{3.5.2}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-17/601c94dd-2c2c-4440-beb4-01d26aedce94/6e5a85aabedb4c33e91764b5e7685b67955ec059b98b364b5128e7dad6142252.jpg)



圖 3.5.2


一長度為L之閉曲線所圍的面積可利用線積分來表示

$$
A = \int_ {0} ^ {L} x d y = \int_ {0} ^ {L} x y ^ {\prime} d s = - \int_ {0} ^ {L} y x ^ {\prime} d s = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {L} (x y ^ {\prime} - x ^ {\prime} y) d s\tag{3.5.3}
$$

將此公式應用到 $C$ 與 $\bar{C}$ 得

$$
